File: 1_fetch_icons.py

# 1_fetch_icons.py
import os
import re
import urllib.request
import urllib.error

ICONS_DIR = os.path.join("assets", "icons")
os.makedirs(ICONS_DIR, exist_ok=True)

BASE_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/FortAwesome/Font-Awesome/6.x/svgs/{style}/{name}.svg"

ICONS_TO_FETCH = {
    "brands": [
        "twitch", "youtube", "telegram", "vk", "discord", "tiktok", "steam", "google"
    ],
    "regular": [
        "copy", "handshake", "clock"
    ],
    "solid": [
        "play", "pause", "forward-step", "backward-step", 
        ("volume-up", "volume-high"), 
        "music", "terminal", "video", "users", "comment-dots", "quote-left", 
        "satellite-dish", "circle-notch", "star", "folder-open", "list", 
        ("search", "magnifying-glass"), 
        ("sort-alpha-down", "arrow-down-a-z"), 
        ("sort-alpha-up", "arrow-up-a-z"), 
        ("sort-numeric-down-alt", "arrow-down-1-9"), 
        ("sort-numeric-up-alt", "arrow-up-1-9"), 
        ("th-large", "table-cells-large"), ("th", "table-cells"), 
        ("trash-alt", "trash-can"), 
        "chevron-up", "chevron-down", "chevron-right", "database", 
        ("times", "xmark"), ("cog", "gear"), "skull", "eye", "headphones", 
        ("sync-alt", "rotate-right"), "heart", "arrow-up", "sack-dollar", 
        "ghost", "bolt", "microchip", "desktop", "memory", "microphone-lines", 
        ("hard-hat", "helmet-safety"), ("broadcast-tower", "tower-broadcast"), 
        ("history", "clock-rotate-left"), "grip-vertical", "plus", "file-code", 
        "camera", "pen", "gamepad", "film", ("calendar-times", "calendar-xmark"), 
        "laptop-code", "code", "sun", "wave-square", "dragon", "fire", 
        ("beer", "beer-mug-empty"), "medal", "tree", "campground", "city", 
        ("user-friends", "user-group"), ("map-marker-alt", "location-dot"), 
        "hammer", "user", "fish", "water" # Добавлены fish и water для Tetlabot
    ]
}

def fetch_icons():
    print(f"Перезагрузка полноценных SVG в {ICONS_DIR}...\n")
    
    for style, icons in ICONS_TO_FETCH.items():
        for icon in icons:
            if isinstance(icon, tuple):
                save_name, fetch_name = icon
            else:
                save_name, fetch_name = icon, icon

            url = BASE_URL.format(style=style, name=fetch_name)
            filepath = os.path.join(ICONS_DIR, f"{save_name}.svg")

            try:
                req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req) as response:
                    svg_content = response.read().decode('utf-8')
                    
                    # ПРАВИЛЬНОЕ удаление комментариев без повреждения тега <svg>
                    svg_content = re.sub(r'<!--.*?-->', '', svg_content, flags=re.DOTALL).strip()

                    with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        f.write(svg_content)
                
                print(f"[OK] {save_name}.svg")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ERROR] Не удалось скачать {save_name}: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    fetch_icons()
    print("\nГотово! Теперь запустите 2_bundle_icons.py")

================================================================================
File: 2_bundle_icons.py
================================================================================

# 2_bundle_icons.py
import os
import glob
import re

ICONS_DIR = os.path.join("assets", "icons")
OUTPUT_JS = os.path.join("js", "utils", "icons.js")

def bundle_icons():
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(OUTPUT_JS), exist_ok=True)
    svg_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(ICONS_DIR, "*.svg"))
    
    if not svg_files:
        print(f"Иконки не найдены в {ICONS_DIR}. Сначала запустите 1_fetch_icons.py")
        return

    icons_dict = []
    print(f"Сборка {len(svg_files)} иконок в {OUTPUT_JS}...")

    for filepath in svg_files:
        filename = os.path.basename(filepath)
        icon_name = os.path.splitext(filename)[0]

        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            svg_content = f.read().strip()
            
            # Гарантируем наличие width, height и currentColor
            if 'width=' not in svg_content:
                svg_content = svg_content.replace('<svg ', '<svg width="1em" height="1em" ')
            else:
                svg_content = re.sub(r'width="[^"]+"', 'width="1em"', svg_content)
                svg_content = re.sub(r'height="[^"]+"', 'height="1em"', svg_content)
            
            if 'fill=' not in svg_content:
                svg_content = svg_content.replace('<svg ', '<svg fill="currentColor" ')
            else:
                svg_content = re.sub(r'fill="[^"]+"', 'fill="currentColor"', svg_content)

            svg_content = svg_content.replace('`', '\\`')
            icons_dict.append(f"    '{icon_name}': `{svg_content}`")

    js_content = """/* js/utils/icons.js */
/* АВТОГЕНЕРАЦИЯ: Не редактировать вручную. Используйте 2_bundle_icons.py */

export const Icons = {
""" + ",\n".join(icons_dict) + """
};

/**
 * Ищет все теги <i> с классами FontAwesome и вставляет внутрь полноценный SVG
 */
export function replaceIcons(root = document) {
    const iconEls = root.querySelectorAll('i[class*="fa-"]:not(.icon-replaced)');
    
    iconEls.forEach(el => {
        el.classList.add('icon-replaced');
        const classes = Array.from(el.classList);
        
        const faClass = classes.find(c => c.startsWith('fa-') && !['fa-spin', 'fa-pulse', 'fa-fw'].includes(c));
        if (!faClass) return;
        
        const iconName = faClass.replace('fa-', '');
        const svgString = Icons[iconName];
        
        if (svgString) {
            el.innerHTML = svgString;
            
            if (classes.includes('fa-spin')) {
                el.classList.add('icon-spin');
            }
        }
    });
}
"""
    with open(OUTPUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(js_content)

    print("\nБандл успешно пересобран!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    bundle_icons()

@import 'variables.css';

/* =========================================
   GLOBAL KEYFRAMES (КОНСОЛИДИРОВАННЫЕ АНИМАЦИИ)
   ========================================= */

@keyframes pulse { 
    0% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; } 
    50% { transform: scale(1.2); opacity: 0.7; } 
    100% { transform: scale(1); opacity: 1; } 
}

    0%, 100% { opacity: 1; } 
    50% { opacity: 0; } 
}

@keyframes rotate-simple { 
    from { transform: rotate(0deg); } 
    to { transform: rotate(360deg); } 
}

    0% { background-position: 0% 50%; } 
    100% { background-position: 200% 50%; } 
}

    to { background-position: 600px 600px, -400px 800px; }
}

    0% { background-position: 0 0; } 
    100% { background-position: 0 100%; } 
}

/* js/agents-controller.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';
import { AgentCard } from './components/AgentCard.js';

export class AgentsController {
    constructor() {
        this.container = document.getElementById('route-agents');
        if (!this.container) return;

        this.els = {
            page: document.getElementById('agents-page'),
            backBtn: document.getElementById('btn-agents-back'),
            bg: document.getElementById('agents-cinematic-bg'),
            detailZone: document.getElementById('agent-detail-zone'),
            
            avatar: document.getElementById('agent-big-avatar'),
            name: document.getElementById('agent-name'),
            role: document.getElementById('agent-role'),
            lvlBadge: document.getElementById('agent-lvl-badge').querySelector('span'),
            timeBadge: document.getElementById('agent-time-badge').querySelector('span'),
            levelFill: document.getElementById('agent-level-fill'),
            desc: document.getElementById('agent-desc'),
            
            listContainer: document.getElementById('agents-scroll-list'),
            totalCount: document.getElementById('agents-total-count')
        };

        this.agentsData = [];
        this.currentAgentId = null;

        this.bindEvents();
    }

    mount(targetId) {
        console.log('👤 [Agents] Компонент смонтирован');
        this.loadDataAndRender(targetId);
    }

    unmount() {
        console.log('👤 [Agents] Компонент размонтирован');
    }

    bindEvents() {
        this.els.backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            AppRouter.navigate('/');
        });
    }

    loadDataAndRender(targetId) {
        const state = AppStore.getState();
        
        if (!state.data.isAgentsLoaded) {
            const unsubscribe = AppStore.subscribe((newState) => {
                if (newState.data.isAgentsLoaded) {
                    unsubscribe();
                    this.loadDataAndRender(targetId);
                }
            });
            return;
        }

        this.agentsData = state.data.agents;
        
        if (!this.agentsData || this.agentsData.length === 0) {
            console.warn('[Agents] База агентов пуста.');
            return;
        }

        this.renderList();

        // Выбираем запрошенного агента, либо первого в списке
        let agent = this.agentsData.find(a => a.id === targetId || a.name === targetId);
        if (!agent) {
            agent = this.agentsData[0];
            // Беззвучно обновляем URL на правильного агента
            AppRouter.navigate('/agents', { id: agent.id });
        } else {
            this.renderDetail(agent);
        }
    }

    renderList() {
        this.els.totalCount.textContent = this.agentsData.length;
        
        this.els.listContainer.innerHTML = this.agentsData.map(agent => {
            const colorVal = AgentCard.getRoleColor(agent.color);
            const { level } = AgentCard.calculateLevelData(agent.createdAt);
            
            const avatarContent = agent.avatarPath || agent.image 
                ? `<img src="${agent.avatarPath || agent.image}" onerror="this.style.display='none'">` 
                : (agent.customHtml || `<i class="${agent.mainIcon || 'fas fa-user'}"></i>`);

            const displayName = agent.squadName || agent.name;

            return `
                <div class="agent-list-card" data-id="${agent.id}" style="--card-color: ${colorVal}">
                    <div class="alc-avatar">${avatarContent}</div>
                    <div class="alc-info">
                        <div class="alc-name">${displayName}</div>
                        <div class="alc-role">${agent.role || 'Агент'}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="alc-lvl">LVL.${level}</div>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');

        this.els.listContainer.querySelectorAll('.agent-list-card').forEach(card => {
            card.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                AppRouter.navigate('/agents', { id: card.dataset.id });
            });
        });
    }

    renderDetail(agent) {
        if (!agent) return;
        this.currentAgentId = agent.id;

        // 1. Обновляем классы активности в списке
        this.els.listContainer.querySelectorAll('.agent-list-card').forEach(card => {
            card.classList.toggle('active', card.dataset.id === agent.id);
        });

        // 2. Анимация Глитча
        this.els.detailZone.classList.remove('is-glitching');
        void this.els.detailZone.offsetWidth; // Reflow
        this.els.detailZone.classList.add('is-glitching');

        setTimeout(() => {
            const colorVal = AgentCard.getRoleColor(agent.color);
            const { level, progress, daysActive } = AgentCard.calculateLevelData(agent.createdAt);

            // Обновляем CSS-переменную для всего экрана
            this.els.page.style.setProperty('--agent-color', colorVal);

            // Тексты
            this.els.name.textContent = agent.squadName || agent.name;
            this.els.role.textContent = agent.role || 'Агент';
            this.els.lvlBadge.textContent = `LVL.${level}`;
            this.els.timeBadge.textContent = agent.createdAt ? `${daysActive} дн. в сети` : `ДАННЫЕ ЗАСЕКРЕЧЕНЫ`;
            this.els.desc.innerHTML = agent.description || 'Информация о данном агенте отсутствует в базе.';

            // Прогресс бар
            this.els.levelFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;

            // Аватарка
            const avatarContent = agent.avatarPath || agent.image 
                ? `<img src="${agent.avatarPath || agent.image}" onerror="this.style.display='none'">` 
                : (agent.customHtml || `<i class="${agent.mainIcon || 'fas fa-user'}"></i>`);
            this.els.avatar.innerHTML = avatarContent;

        }, 150);
    }
}

/* js/api.js */
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';
import { DATA_REGISTRY } from './data-registry.js';

/**
 * Универсальная функция загрузки JSON с умным Fallback-ом.
 * Работает одинаково стабильно и на сервере (HTTP), и локально (file://).
 * 
 * @param {string} endpoint - Имя файла (напр. 'schedule.json')
 * @param {any} fallbackData - Данные по умолчанию
 */
export async function loadData(endpoint, fallbackData = []) {
    const isLocalFile = window.location.protocol === 'file:';

    // 1. Оффлайн/Локальный режим: сразу отдаем данные из встроенного реестра
    if (isLocalFile) {
        console.log(`[API] Протокол file:// обнаружен. Данные взяты из Data Registry: ${endpoint}`);
        return DATA_REGISTRY[endpoint] || fallbackData;
    }

    // 2. Web-сервер режим: пробуем загрузить свежий JSON через fetch
    const paths = [
        `${AppConfig.api.dataBaseUrl}${endpoint}`,
        `./data/${endpoint}`,
        `data/${endpoint}`
    ];

    for (const path of paths) {
        try {
            const controller = new AbortController();
            const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 5000);
            
            const response = await fetch(`${path}?t=${Date.now()}`, { signal: controller.signal });
            clearTimeout(timeoutId);
            
            if (response.ok) {
                const data = await response.json();
                console.log(`[API] Загружено через HTTP Fetch: ${path}`);
                return data;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn(`⚠️ [API] Не удалось загрузить ${path}:`, error.message);
        }
    }
    
    // 3. Fallback: если сервер упал, но сайт открыт по http
    console.warn(`❌ [API] Все сетевые попытки провалились для ${endpoint}. Использую Data Registry.`);
    return DATA_REGISTRY[endpoint] || fallbackData;
}

/* js/app.js */

import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js'; 
import { AppStore } from './store.js'; 
import { AppRouter } from './router.js'; 

import { getDashboardView } from './views/dashboardView.js';
import { getTierlistView } from './views/tierlistView.js';
import { getMediaItemView } from './views/mediaItemView.js';
import { getLibraryView } from './views/libraryView.js';
import { getYoutubeLibraryView } from './views/youtubeLibraryView.js';
// ДОБАВЛЕНО: Новый View для Агентов
import { getAgentsView } from './views/agentsView.js';

import { SynthEngine } from './services/SynthEngine.js'; 
import { ConfigInjector } from './services/ConfigInjector.js';
import { MobileOptimizer } from './services/MobileOptimizer.js'; 
import { FXController } from './FXController.js'; 

import { TerminalController } from './terminal-core.js';
import { SettingsMenu } from './settings-menu.js';
import { registerTerminalCommands } from './terminal-commands.js'; 

import { initScrollPhysics } from './ui/scroll-physics.js';
import { initCryptoWidget } from './ui/crypto-widget.js';
import { initNavRail } from './ui/nav-rail.js';
import { initSpecs } from './ui/SpecsRenderer.js'; 

import { initMediaArchive } from './media-manager.js';
import { HeroController } from './hero-controller.js';
import { SquadController } from './squad-controller.js';

import { ScheduleManager } from './schedule.js';
import { SubscribersManager } from './subscribers.js';
import { HubController } from './hub-controller.js';
import { StatsManager } from './stats.js'; 
import { initMusicPlayer, toggleMusicMode } from './music-player.js';
import { replaceIcons } from './utils/icons.js'; 

import { TierlistController } from './tierlist/TierlistController.js';
import { MediaItemController } from './media-item-controller.js';
import { LibraryController } from './library-controller.js';
import { YoutubeLibraryController } from './youtube-library-controller.js';
// ДОБАВЛЕНО: Новый контроллер для Агентов
import { AgentsController } from './agents-controller.js';

let terminalCtrl;
let scheduleMgr;
let statsMgr; 
let tierlistCtrl;
let mediaItemCtrl;
let libraryCtrl; 
let youtubeLibCtrl; 
let agentsCtrl;

window.navRailInstance = null;

let dashboardScrollPosition = 0;
let libraryScrollPosition = 0;
let youtubeScrollPosition = 0;
let agentsScrollPosition = 0;

function bootstrap() {
    if (window.AppState && window.AppState.initialized) return;
    
    try {
        console.log(`🚀 [Boot] Запуск системы ${AppConfig.system.osName} ${AppConfig.system.osVersion}...`);
        
        const appRoot = document.getElementById('app');
        if (appRoot) {
            appRoot.innerHTML = `
                <div id="route-dashboard">${getDashboardView()}</div>
                <div id="route-library" style="display: none;">${getLibraryView()}</div>
                <div id="route-youtube" style="display: none;">${getYoutubeLibraryView()}</div>
                <div id="route-agents" style="display: none;">${getAgentsView()}</div>
                <div id="route-tierlist" style="display: none;">${getTierlistView()}</div>
                <div id="route-media-item" style="display: none;">${getMediaItemView()}</div>
                <div id="modals-root"></div>
            `;
            replaceIcons(appRoot);
            console.log('🏗️ [Boot] DOM структура смонтирована (SPA Mode)');
        }

        const iconObserver = new MutationObserver(() => replaceIcons(document.body));
        iconObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

        new SynthEngine(); 
        terminalCtrl = new TerminalController();
        const fxController = new FXController();
        
        if (!MobileOptimizer.isMobile()) {
            new SettingsMenu();
            initScrollPhysics();
            window.navRailInstance = initNavRail();
        }
        
        initCryptoWidget();
        initSpecs(); 
        ConfigInjector.init(); 
        
        new HeroController();
        new SquadController();
        
        fxController.init();
        registerGlobalEvents();
        MobileOptimizer.applyMobilePatches(terminalCtrl, fxController);

        scheduleMgr = new ScheduleManager();
        const subsMgr = new SubscribersManager();
        statsMgr = new StatsManager();
        new HubController();
        
        tierlistCtrl = new TierlistController();
        mediaItemCtrl = new MediaItemController();
        libraryCtrl = new LibraryController(); 
        youtubeLibCtrl = new YoutubeLibraryController(); 
        agentsCtrl = new AgentsController();

        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            const currentPath = state.route.path;
            const prevPath = prevState?.route?.path;

            if (currentPath !== prevPath) {
                const dashEl = document.getElementById('route-dashboard');
                const tierEl = document.getElementById('route-tierlist');
                const itemEl = document.getElementById('route-media-item');
                const libEl = document.getElementById('route-library'); 
                const ytLibEl = document.getElementById('route-youtube'); 
                const agentsEl = document.getElementById('route-agents'); 
                
                if (prevPath === '/' || prevPath === '/archive') dashboardScrollPosition = window.scrollY;
                if (prevPath === '/library') libraryScrollPosition = window.scrollY;
                if (prevPath === '/youtube') youtubeScrollPosition = window.scrollY;
                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/agents')) agentsScrollPosition = window.scrollY;

                dashEl.style.display = 'none';
                tierEl.style.display = 'none';
                itemEl.style.display = 'none';
                libEl.style.display = 'none'; 
                ytLibEl.style.display = 'none';
                agentsEl.style.display = 'none';
                document.body.classList.remove('route-dashboard', 'route-tierlist', 'route-item', 'route-library', 'route-youtube', 'route-agents');

                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/tierlist')) tierlistCtrl.unmount();
                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/item')) mediaItemCtrl.unmount();
                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/library')) libraryCtrl.unmount();
                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/youtube')) youtubeLibCtrl.unmount();
                if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/agents')) agentsCtrl.unmount();

                if (currentPath.startsWith('/tierlist')) {
                    document.body.classList.add('route-tierlist');
                    tierEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: true });
                    tierlistCtrl.mount();
                    window.scrollTo(0, 0);

                } else if (currentPath.startsWith('/item')) {
                    document.body.classList.add('route-item');
                    itemEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: true });
                    mediaItemCtrl.mount(state.route.params.id);

                } else if (currentPath.startsWith('/library')) { 
                    document.body.classList.add('route-library');
                    libEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: true });
                    libraryCtrl.mount();
                    setTimeout(() => window.scrollTo(0, libraryScrollPosition), 10);

                } else if (currentPath.startsWith('/youtube')) { 
                    document.body.classList.add('route-youtube');
                    ytLibEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: true });
                    youtubeLibCtrl.mount();
                    setTimeout(() => window.scrollTo(0, youtubeScrollPosition), 10);

                } else if (currentPath.startsWith('/agents')) { 
                    document.body.classList.add('route-agents');
                    agentsEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: true });
                    agentsCtrl.mount(state.route.params.id);
                    // Восстанавливаем скролл, если мы просто переключились на другого агента, и сбрасываем, если пришли извне
                    if (prevPath && prevPath.startsWith('/agents')) {
                        setTimeout(() => window.scrollTo(0, agentsScrollPosition), 10);
                    } else {
                        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
                    }

                } else {
                    document.body.classList.add('route-dashboard');
                    dashEl.style.display = 'block';
                    AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_SET_PAUSE', payload: false });
                    window.scrollTo(0, dashboardScrollPosition);

                    if (window.navRailInstance) {
                        requestAnimationFrame(() => window.navRailInstance.updatePositions());
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        AppRouter.init();

        scheduleMgr.init().catch(e => console.warn('Ошибка загрузки расписания', e));
        subsMgr.init().catch(e => console.warn('Ошибка загрузки агентов', e));
        statsMgr.init().catch(e => console.warn('Ошибка загрузки статистики Twitch', e));
        initMediaArchive().catch(e => console.warn('Ошибка загрузки базы медиа', e));
        initMusicPlayer().catch(e => console.warn('Ошибка инициализации плеера', e));

        if (window.globalSyncInterval) clearInterval(window.globalSyncInterval);
        window.globalSyncInterval = setInterval(async () => {
            scheduleMgr.init();
            statsMgr.init(); 
            EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "<span style='color:#555'>[SYS] Background data sync complete.</span>" });
        }, 300000);
        
        window.AppState = { initialized: true };
        console.log('✅ [Boot] Инициализация завершена успешно!');
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ [Boot] Критическая ошибка при инициализации:', error);
    }
}

function registerGlobalEvents() {
    EventBus.on('CMD_CLEAR', () => { if (terminalCtrl && terminalCtrl.historyEl) terminalCtrl.historyEl.innerHTML = ''; });
    EventBus.on('CMD_STATUS', () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: 'СИСТЕМЫ В НОРМЕ.' }));
    EventBus.on('CMD_MUSIC', () => EventBus.emit('UI_CLICK_MUSIC'));
    EventBus.on('CMD_PLAYER', () => EventBus.emit('UI_CLICK_MUSIC'));
    
    registerTerminalCommands();
    EventBus.on('UI_CLICK_MUSIC', () => toggleMusicMode());
    EventBus.on('UI_CLICK_REFRESH', () => { scheduleMgr.init(); statsMgr.init(); });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.shiftKey && e.code === 'KeyT' && !document.body.classList.contains('modal-open')) {
            if (document.activeElement.tagName !== 'INPUT' && document.activeElement.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') {
                AppRouter.navigate('/tierlist');
            }
        }
    });
}

window.addEventListener('error', e => console.error('🚨 [System] Uncaught Error:', e.error));

if (document.readyState === 'loading') { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', bootstrap); } 
else { bootstrap(); }

export function addLogLine(html, isTyping = false, forceScroll = false) { if (terminalCtrl) return terminalCtrl.addLogLine(html, isTyping, forceScroll); }
export function setSystemNoiseState(isEnabled) { if (terminalCtrl) terminalCtrl.setSystemNoiseState(isEnabled); }

/* js/audio-core.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';

export class AudioCore {
    constructor() {
        this.audio = new Audio();
        this.audio.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
        this.audio.volume = 0.5;

        this.tracks = []; 
        this.currentTrackIdx = 0;
        this.isPlaying = false;

        this.audioCtx = null;
        this.analyser = null;
        this.source = null;
        this.dataArray = null;

        this.initListeners();
    }

    async loadTracks() {
        // Берем треки напрямую из конфига, без попыток fetch-запросов к music.json
        this.tracks = AppConfig.audio.fallbackTracks || [];
        console.log(`🎵 [AudioCore] Загружено треков из конфига: ${this.tracks.length}`);
        return Promise.resolve();
    }

    initListeners() {
        this.audio.addEventListener('ended', () => this.next());
        this.audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => {
            EventBus.emit('AUDIO_TIME_UPDATE', { 
                currentTime: this.audio.currentTime, 
                duration: this.audio.duration 
            });
        });
    }

    initContext() {
        if (this.audioCtx) return;
        try {
            const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
            this.audioCtx = new AudioContext();
            this.analyser = this.audioCtx.createAnalyser();
            this.analyser.fftSize = 64;
            
            this.source = this.audioCtx.createMediaElementSource(this.audio);
            this.source.connect(this.analyser);
            this.analyser.connect(this.audioCtx.destination);
            
            this.dataArray = new Uint8Array(this.analyser.frequencyBinCount);
            console.log("🎵 [AudioCore] Web Audio API подключен для визуализатора");
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("❌ [AudioCore] Ошибка Web Audio API:", e);
        }
    }

    loadTrack(index) {
        if (!this.tracks || this.tracks.length === 0) return;
        this.currentTrackIdx = index;
        this.audio.src = this.tracks[index].url;
        this.audio.load();
        EventBus.emit('AUDIO_TRACK_LOADED', index);
    }

    togglePlay() {
        this.initContext();
        if (this.audioCtx && this.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') this.audioCtx.resume();
        
        if (this.isPlaying) {
            this.pause();
        } else {
            if (!this.audio.src && this.tracks.length > 0) this.loadTrack(this.currentTrackIdx);
            this.play();
        }
    }

    play() {
        if (!this.tracks || this.tracks.length === 0) return;
        this.audio.play().catch(e => console.error("❌ [AudioCore] Ошибка Play:", e));
        this.isPlaying = true;
        EventBus.emit('AUDIO_PLAY_STATE_CHANGED', true);
        
        EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'PLAYING', value: this.tracks[this.currentTrackIdx].title, color: 'var(--neon-pink)' });
    }

    pause() {
        this.audio.pause();
        this.isPlaying = false;
        EventBus.emit('AUDIO_PLAY_STATE_CHANGED', false);
        
        EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'STATUS', value: 'PAUSED', color: '#888' });
    }

    next() {
        if (!this.tracks || this.tracks.length === 0) return;
        let nextIdx = (this.currentTrackIdx + 1) % this.tracks.length;
        this.loadTrack(nextIdx);
        this.play();
        EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'CMD', value: 'NEXT_TRACK >>', color: 'var(--neon-pink)' });
    }

    prev() {
        if (!this.tracks || this.tracks.length === 0) return;
        let prevIdx = (this.currentTrackIdx - 1 + this.tracks.length) % this.tracks.length;
        this.loadTrack(prevIdx);
        this.play();
        EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'CMD', value: '<< PREV_TRACK', color: 'var(--neon-pink)' });
    }

    setVolume(vol) { this.audio.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)); }
    seek(time) { if (!isNaN(this.audio.duration)) this.audio.currentTime = time; }

    getFrequencyData() {
        if (!this.isPlaying || !this.analyser || !this.dataArray) return null;
        this.analyser.getByteFrequencyData(this.dataArray);
        return this.dataArray;
    }
}

================================================================================

/* js/audio-ui.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';

const VOLUME_TICKS_COUNT = 20;

export class AudioUI {
    constructor(audioCore) {
        this.core = audioCore;
        this.isMusicModeActive = false;
        
        this.els = {
            squadView: document.getElementById('squad-view'),
            musicView: document.getElementById('music-view'),
            liveStatus: document.getElementById('live-status-container'),
            musicControls: document.getElementById('music-controls-container'),
            playlist: document.getElementById('playlist-list'),
            btnPlay: document.getElementById('btn-play'),
            btnPrev: document.getElementById('btn-prev'),
            btnNext: document.getElementById('btn-next'),
            hudRight: document.querySelector('.hud-side-panel.right'),
            progressArea: document.getElementById('progress-area'),
            progressFill: document.getElementById('progress-fill'),
            volumeTicksContainer: document.getElementById('volume-ticks')
        };

        this.init();
    }

    init() {
        if (!this.els.playlist) return;

        this.renderPlaylist();
        if (this.els.volumeTicksContainer) {
            this.createVolumeTicks();
            this.updateVolumeUI(this.core.audio.volume);
        }
        this.bindEvents();
    }

    bindEvents() {
        if (this.els.btnPlay) this.els.btnPlay.addEventListener('click', () => this.core.togglePlay());
        if (this.els.btnPrev) this.els.btnPrev.addEventListener('click', () => { this.core.initContext(); this.core.prev(); });
        if (this.els.btnNext) this.els.btnNext.addEventListener('click', () => { this.core.initContext(); this.core.next(); });
        if (this.els.playlist) this.els.playlist.addEventListener('click', () => this.core.initContext());

        if (this.els.progressArea) {
            this.els.progressArea.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (!this.isMusicModeActive || isNaN(this.core.audio.duration)) return;
                const clickX = e.offsetX;
                const newTime = (clickX / this.els.progressArea.clientWidth) * this.core.audio.duration;
                this.core.seek(newTime);
                
                const timeStr = `${Math.floor(newTime/60)}:${Math.floor(newTime%60).toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'SEEK_TO', value: timeStr, color: '#ccc' });
            });
        }

        if (this.els.volumeTicksContainer) {
            let isDraggingVolume = false;
            this.els.volumeTicksContainer.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                isDraggingVolume = true;
                this.handleVolumeInput(e);
            });
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (isDraggingVolume) this.handleVolumeInput(e);
            });
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                if (isDraggingVolume && this.isMusicModeActive) {
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'VOL_UPDATE', value: `${Math.round(this.core.audio.volume * 100)}%`, color: '#ccc' });
                }
                isDraggingVolume = false;
            });
        }

        EventBus.on('AUDIO_PLAY_STATE_CHANGED', (isPlaying) => {
            if (this.els.btnPlay) {
                const icon = this.els.btnPlay.querySelector('i');
                if (icon) icon.className = isPlaying ? 'fas fa-pause' : 'fas fa-play';
            }
            this.renderPlaylist();
        });

        EventBus.on('AUDIO_TRACK_LOADED', () => this.renderPlaylist());

        EventBus.on('AUDIO_TIME_UPDATE', ({ currentTime, duration }) => {
            if (isNaN(duration) || !this.els.progressFill) return;
            this.els.progressFill.style.width = `${(currentTime / duration) * 100}%`;
        });

        // Слушаем центральное хранилище вместо EventBus напрямую
        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            if (state.audio.isMusicModeActive !== prevState?.audio?.isMusicModeActive) {
                this.toggleMode(state.audio.isMusicModeActive);
            }
        });
    }

    handleVolumeInput(e) {
        if (!this.isMusicModeActive || !this.els.volumeTicksContainer) return;
        const rect = this.els.volumeTicksContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
        let x = Math.max(0, Math.min(e.clientX - rect.left, rect.width));
        const step = 1 / VOLUME_TICKS_COUNT;
        let newVolume = Math.round((x / rect.width) / step) * step;
        
        this.core.setVolume(newVolume);
        this.updateVolumeUI(newVolume);
    }

    createVolumeTicks() {
        if (!this.els.volumeTicksContainer) return;
        this.els.volumeTicksContainer.innerHTML = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < VOLUME_TICKS_COUNT; i++) {
            const tick = document.createElement('div');
            tick.className = 'vol-tick';
            this.els.volumeTicksContainer.appendChild(tick);
        }
    }

    updateVolumeUI(vol) {
        if (!this.els.volumeTicksContainer) return;
        const ticks = this.els.volumeTicksContainer.children;
        const activeCount = Math.round(vol * VOLUME_TICKS_COUNT);
        for (let i = 0; i < ticks.length; i++) {
            ticks[i].classList.toggle('active', i < activeCount);
        }
    }

    renderPlaylist() {
        const tracks = this.core.tracks;
        if (!tracks || tracks.length === 0) {
            if (this.els.playlist) this.els.playlist.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:15px; color:#666; text-align:center;">Плейлист пуст</div>';
            return;
        }

        if (this.els.playlist) {
            this.els.playlist.innerHTML = tracks.map((track, index) => `
                <div class="track-item ${index === this.core.currentTrackIdx ? 'active' : ''}" data-index="${index}">
                    <div class="track-icon">
                        ${index === this.core.currentTrackIdx && this.core.isPlaying ? '<i class="fas fa-volume-up"></i>' : '<i class="fas fa-music"></i>'}
                    </div>
                    <div class="track-info">
                        <span class="track-title">${track.title}</span>
                        <span class="track-artist">${track.artist}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');

            this.els.playlist.querySelectorAll('.track-item').forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    this.core.initContext();
                    this.core.loadTrack(parseInt(item.dataset.index));
                    this.core.play();
                });
            });
        }
    }

    toggleMode(isActive) {
        if (this.isMusicModeActive === isActive) return;
        const panelContent = document.querySelector('.hud-side-panel.right .panel-content');
        const statusContainer = document.querySelector('.main-display-status');
        
        if (!panelContent || panelContent.classList.contains('is-glitching')) return;

        panelContent.classList.add('is-glitching');
        if (statusContainer) statusContainer.classList.add('status-switching');

        setTimeout(() => {
            this.isMusicModeActive = isActive;
            
            EventBus.emit('SYS_SET_NOISE', !this.isMusicModeActive);
            // Извещаем визуализатор напрямую через шину событий (пока что)
            EventBus.emit('STATE_MUSIC_CHANGED', this.isMusicModeActive);

            if (this.isMusicModeActive) {
                if (this.els.squadView) this.els.squadView.style.display = 'none';
                if (this.els.musicView) this.els.musicView.style.display = 'flex';
                if (this.els.liveStatus) this.els.liveStatus.style.display = 'none';
                if (this.els.musicControls) this.els.musicControls.style.display = 'flex';
                if (this.els.hudRight) this.els.hudRight.classList.add('music-mode-active');
                
                this.updateVolumeUI(this.core.audio.volume);
                this.renderPlaylist();
                
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'MODULE_INIT', value: 'READY', color: 'var(--neon-pink)', forceScroll: true });
            } else {
                if (this.core.isPlaying) this.core.pause();
                
                if (this.els.squadView) this.els.squadView.style.display = 'flex';
                if (this.els.musicView) this.els.musicView.style.display = 'none';
                if (this.els.liveStatus) this.els.liveStatus.style.display = 'flex';
                if (this.els.musicControls) this.els.musicControls.style.display = 'none';
                if (this.els.hudRight) this.els.hudRight.classList.remove('music-mode-active');
                if (this.els.progressFill) this.els.progressFill.style.width = '0%';
                
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'MUSIC', action: 'MODULE', value: 'DEACTIVATED', color: '#888', forceScroll: true });
            }
            
            if (statusContainer) statusContainer.classList.remove('status-switching');
        }, 200);

        setTimeout(() => panelContent.classList.remove('is-glitching'), 400);
    }
}

/* js/audio-visualizer.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';

export class AudioVisualizer {
    constructor(audioCore) {
        this.core = audioCore;
        this.animationId = null;
        this.isActive = false;

        this.ringRot1 = 0;
        this.ringRot2 = 0;
        this.ringRot3 = 0;
        
        // Сглаженные переменные для плавности анимации
        this.smoothedBeat = 0;
        this.smoothedMarkers = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0];

        this.els = {
            ringsContainer: document.querySelector('.main-display-rings'),
            mainAvatar: document.querySelector('.main-display-avatar'),
            ring1: document.querySelector('.ring-1'),
            ring2: document.querySelector('.ring-2'),
            ring3: document.querySelector('.ring-3')
        };

        this.injectSeamlessKeyframes();
        this.initListeners();
    }

    injectSeamlessKeyframes() {
        if (document.getElementById('seamless-rotation-style')) return;
        const style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = 'seamless-rotation-style';
        style.innerHTML = `
            @keyframes rotate-seamless {
                from { transform: rotate(var(--rot-start, 0deg)); }
                to { transform: rotate(calc(var(--rot-start, 0deg) + 360deg)); }
            }
        `;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
    }

    initListeners() {
        EventBus.on('STATE_MUSIC_CHANGED', (isActive) => {
            this.isActive = isActive;
            if (isActive) {
                this.start();
            } else {
                this.stop();
            }
        });
    }

    getComputedRotation(el) {
        if (!el) return 0;
        const style = window.getComputedStyle(el);
        const transform = style.transform || style.webkitTransform;
        if (transform !== 'none') {
            const values = transform.split('(')[1].split(')')[0].split(',');
            // Убрали Math.round()! Теперь возвращаем точное значение с плавающей точкой.
            // Это полностью избавляет от микро-рывка при перехвате анимации.
            return Math.atan2(values[1], values[0]) * (180 / Math.PI);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    start() {
        this.ringRot1 = this.getComputedRotation(this.els.ring1);
        this.ringRot2 = this.getComputedRotation(this.els.ring2);
        this.ringRot3 = this.getComputedRotation(this.els.ring3);

        // Отключаем CSS анимацию вращения, теперь крутит JS
        [this.els.ring1, this.els.ring2, this.els.ring3].forEach(el => {
            if (el) el.style.animation = 'none';
        });

        // Отключаем "дыхание" контейнера, переводим в режим бита
        if (this.els.ringsContainer) {
            this.els.ringsContainer.classList.add('js-beat-controlled');
        }

        this.smoothedBeat = 0;
        this.smoothedMarkers.fill(0);
        this.loop();
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.animationId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationId);
            this.animationId = null;
        }

        // Сброс аватара
        if (this.els.mainAvatar) this.els.mainAvatar.style.filter = '';
        
        // Возвращаем кольца в стандартный режим дыхания
        if (this.els.ringsContainer) {
            this.els.ringsContainer.style.transform = '';
            this.els.ringsContainer.style.removeProperty('--beat');
            this.els.ringsContainer.classList.remove('js-beat-controlled');
        }

        // Возвращаем вращение обратно CSS ключам (с точки, где остановил JS)
        if (this.els.ring1) {
            this.els.ring1.style.setProperty('--rot-start', `${this.ringRot1 % 360}deg`);
            this.els.ring1.style.animationName = 'rotate-seamless';
            this.els.ring1.style.transform = '';
        }
        if (this.els.ring2) {
            this.els.ring2.style.setProperty('--rot-start', `${this.ringRot2 % 360}deg`);
            this.els.ring2.style.animationName = 'rotate-seamless';
            this.els.ring2.style.transform = '';
        }
        if (this.els.ring3) {
            this.els.ring3.style.setProperty('--rot-start', `${this.ringRot3 % 360}deg`);
            this.els.ring3.style.animationName = 'rotate-seamless';
            this.els.ring3.style.transform = '';
        }

        // Сброс маркеров луча
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-marker .nav-shape').forEach(shape => {
            shape.style.removeProperty('--audio-scale');
            shape.style.removeProperty('--audio-glow');
            shape.style.removeProperty('--audio-color');
        });
    }

    loop() {
        if (!this.isActive) return;
        this.animationId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.loop());

        const dataArray = this.core.getFrequencyData();
        
        if (!dataArray) {
            if (this.els.mainAvatar) this.els.mainAvatar.style.filter = '';
            if (this.els.ringsContainer) {
                this.els.ringsContainer.style.removeProperty('--beat');
                this.els.ringsContainer.style.transform = '';
            }
            return;
        }

        // --- ВЫЧИСЛЕНИЯ БАСА ---
        let bassTotal = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) bassTotal += dataArray[i];
        const bassAverage = bassTotal / 8;

        // 1. ИДЕАЛЬНЫЕ ТАНЦУЮЩИЕ МАРКЕРЫ (Сглаживание Lerp)
        const liveShapes = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-marker .nav-shape');
        if (liveShapes.length > 0) {
            const bands = [
                { start: 0, end: 2 },   { start: 3, end: 5 },   
                { start: 6, end: 10 },  { start: 11, end: 16 }, 
                { start: 17, end: 24 }, { start: 25, end: 31 }  
            ];

            liveShapes.forEach((shape, i) => {
                const band = bands[i % bands.length];
                let energy = 0;
                for (let j = band.start; j <= band.end; j++) {
                    energy += (dataArray[j] || 0);
                }
                const rawIntensity = (energy / (band.end - band.start + 1)) / 255;

                const smoothing = rawIntensity > this.smoothedMarkers[i] ? 0.6 : 0.1;
                this.smoothedMarkers[i] += (rawIntensity - this.smoothedMarkers[i]) * smoothing;
                const finalValue = this.smoothedMarkers[i];

                if (finalValue > 0.05) {
                    shape.style.setProperty('--audio-scale', finalValue * 0.8);
                    shape.style.setProperty('--audio-glow', `${finalValue * 25}px`);
                    shape.style.setProperty('--audio-color', `rgba(255, 45, 149, ${0.4 + finalValue * 0.6})`);
                } else {
                    shape.style.setProperty('--audio-scale', 0);
                    shape.style.setProperty('--audio-glow', '0px');
                    shape.style.removeProperty('--audio-color');
                }
            });
        }

        // 2. Аватар
        if (this.els.mainAvatar) {
            const sharpIntensity = Math.pow(bassAverage / 255, 2);
            this.els.mainAvatar.style.filter = `drop-shadow(0 0 ${10 + (sharpIntensity * 35)}px var(--neon-pink))`;
        }

        // 3. НОВАЯ ПЛАВНАЯ ЛОГИКА КОЛЕЦ (ВОЛНА)
        const rawBeat = Math.pow(bassAverage / 255, 2); 
        
        // Линейная интерполяция (Lerp): быстрый взрыв (0.6), плавное затухание (0.1)
        const beatSmoothing = rawBeat > this.smoothedBeat ? 0.6 : 0.1;
        this.smoothedBeat += (rawBeat - this.smoothedBeat) * beatSmoothing;
        
        if (this.els.ringsContainer) {
            // Легкое масштабирование самого контейнера для вайба
            this.els.ringsContainer.style.transform = `scale(${1 + this.smoothedBeat * 0.05})`;
            // ПРОКИДЫВАЕМ СГЛАЖЕННУЮ ПЕРЕМЕННУЮ В CSS
            this.els.ringsContainer.style.setProperty('--beat', this.smoothedBeat.toFixed(3));
        }

        // 4. Вращение
        const rotationSpeed = 0.5 + this.smoothedBeat * 3.0;
        this.ringRot1 += rotationSpeed;
        this.ringRot2 -= rotationSpeed * 1.1;
        this.ringRot3 += rotationSpeed * 0.4;

        // JS крутит кольца без CSS scale, так как scale применяется к родительскому контейнеру
        if (this.els.ring1) this.els.ring1.style.transform = `rotate(${this.ringRot1}deg)`;
        if (this.els.ring2) this.els.ring2.style.transform = `rotate(${this.ringRot2}deg)`;
        if (this.els.ring3) this.els.ring3.style.transform = `rotate(${this.ringRot3}deg)`;
    }
}

/* js/comets.js */

const COMET_COLORS = ['#39ff14', '#ff2d95', '#ff4444', '#ff8c00', '#007bff', '#00ffff', '#ffffff'];

export class CometEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.containerBg = document.getElementById('comet-system');
        this.idleTimeoutId = null;
        this.showerIntervalId = null;
        this.activeComets = new Set();
    }

    startIdle() {
        if (!this.containerBg) return;

        Object.assign(this.containerBg.style, {
            position: 'fixed', /* ИСПРАВЛЕНО: fixed для следования за экраном */
            inset: '0', 
            width: '100%',
            height: '100%',
            zIndex: 'var(--z-bg)', 
            pointerEvents: 'none', 
            overflow: 'hidden'
        });

        this.scheduleNextIdleCycle();
        console.log('🌠 [CometEngine] Фоновый режим запущен');
    }

    stopIdle() {
        if (this.idleTimeoutId) {
            clearTimeout(this.idleTimeoutId);
            this.idleTimeoutId = null;
        }
    }

    triggerShower() {
        if (!this.containerBg) return;
        this.stopShower(); 

        console.log("🌠 [CometEngine] Шторм начат!");
        const duration = 10000;
        const startTime = Date.now();

        this.showerIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
            if (Date.now() - startTime > duration) {
                this.stopShower(); 
                return;
            }
            const batchSize = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1;
            for (let i = 0; i < batchSize; i++) this.spawnComet(null, true);
        }, 100);
    }

    stopShower() {
        if (this.showerIntervalId) {
            clearInterval(this.showerIntervalId);
            this.showerIntervalId = null;
            console.log("🌠 [CometEngine] Шторм окончен");
        }
    }

    scheduleNextIdleCycle() {
        const delay = Math.random() * 30000 + 30000; 
        this.idleTimeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!this.showerIntervalId) {
                const count = Math.random() > 0.7 ? 2 : 1;
                const side = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    setTimeout(() => this.spawnComet(side), i * 300); 
                }
            }
            this.scheduleNextIdleCycle();
        }, delay);
    }

    spawnComet(forcedSide = null, isFast = false) {
        if (!this.containerBg) return;

        const comet = document.createElement('div');
        comet.className = 'comet';
        
        const color = COMET_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * COMET_COLORS.length)];
        comet.style.color = color;
        comet.style.background = `linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, ${color}, #fff)`;
        
        const w = window.innerWidth;
        const h = window.innerHeight;
        const offset = 150; 

        const side = forcedSide !== null ? forcedSide : Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);
        let startX, startY, endX, endY;

        /* ИСПРАВЛЕНО: Больше никаких scrollY. Рисуем поверх экрана. */
        switch(side) {
            case 0: startX = Math.random() * w; startY = -offset; endX = Math.random() * w; endY = h + offset; break;
            case 1: startX = w + offset; startY = Math.random() * h; endX = -offset; endY = Math.random() * h; break;
            case 2: startX = Math.random() * w; startY = h + offset; endX = Math.random() * w; endY = -offset; break;
            case 3: startX = -offset; startY = Math.random() * h; endX = w + offset; endY = Math.random() * h; break;
        }

        const deltaX = endX - startX;
        const deltaY = endY - startY;
        const angle = Math.atan2(deltaY, deltaX) * (180 / Math.PI);

        let speedBase = isFast ? (Math.random() * 0.8 + 1.2) : (Math.random() * 0.3 + 0.2);
        const distance = Math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaY * deltaY);
        const duration = distance / speedBase;

        const length = Math.min(Math.max(speedBase * 300, 150), 600);
        const thickness = (Math.random() * 2 + 1);

        comet.style.width = `${length}px`;
        comet.style.height = `${thickness}px`;

        this.containerBg.appendChild(comet);
        this.activeComets.add(comet);

        const animation = comet.animate([
            { transform: `translate(${startX}px, ${startY}px) rotate(${angle}deg)`, opacity: 0 },
            { transform: `translate(${startX + deltaX * 0.15}px, ${startY + deltaY * 0.15}px) rotate(${angle}deg)`, opacity: 1, offset: 0.1 },
            { transform: `translate(${endX}px, ${endY}px) rotate(${angle}deg)`, opacity: 0 }
        ], { duration: duration, easing: 'linear' });

        animation.onfinish = () => {
            if (comet.parentNode) comet.remove();
            this.activeComets.delete(comet);
        };
    }
}

/* js/dragon-engine.js */

const DRAGON_PALETTES = [
    { name: 'TOXIC_GREEN', main: '#39ff14', dark: '#002200', eyes: '#ccff00' },
    { name: 'NEON_PINK',   main: '#ff2d95', dark: '#2d0015', eyes: '#ffffff' },
    { name: 'CYBER_BLUE',  main: '#00ccff', dark: '#001a33', eyes: '#ffffff' },
    { name: 'ALERT_RED',   main: '#ff4444', dark: '#2b0000', eyes: '#ffff00' },
    { name: 'SOLAR_GOLD',  main: '#ffd700', dark: '#2b2500', eyes: '#ffffff' },
    { name: 'VOID_PURPLE', main: '#bd00ff', dark: '#1a002b', eyes: '#00ffff' }
];

export class DragonEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.svg = null;
        this.rafId = null;
        
        this.pointer = { x: window.innerWidth / 2, y: window.innerHeight / 2 };
        this.rad = 0;

        // FPS Limiter
        this.lastTime = 0;
        this.fpsInterval = 1000 / 60; // Максимум 60 FPS

        this.onPointerMove = this.onPointerMove.bind(this);
    }

    start() {
        if (this.svg) this.stop();

        const palette = DRAGON_PALETTES[Math.floor(Math.random() * DRAGON_PALETTES.length)];
        const N = Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) + 30; 
        const wingIndices = new Set();
        const maxWings = N > 50 ? 4 : 2;
        let currentWingPos = 8; 
        
        for(let k = 0; k < (Math.floor(Math.random() * maxWings) + 1); k++) {
            wingIndices.add(currentWingPos);
            currentWingPos += Math.floor(Math.random() * 7) + 6;
            if (currentWingPos >= N - 10) break;
        }

        const xmlns = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
        const xlinkns = "http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink";
        
        this.svg = document.createElementNS(xmlns, "svg");
        Object.assign(this.svg.style, {
            position: "fixed", /* Оставляем fixed, чтобы не обрезался внизу сайта */
            inset: "0", 
            width: "100%",
            height: "100%", 
            zIndex: "var(--z-system)", 
            pointerEvents: "none", 
            background: "transparent",
            filter: `drop-shadow(0 0 5px ${palette.main}) contrast(1.1)` 
        });

        this.svg.innerHTML = `
            <defs>
                <g id="Cabeza">
                    <path style="fill: ${palette.eyes};" d="M-28.9,-1.1L-28.55 -1.95Q-28.1 -3.1 -27.25 -2.95L-26.7 -2.95Q-27.7 -1.65 -28.9 -1.1M-18.35,-1.8Q-15.1 -10.3 -9.6 -6.05Q-15.1 -6.2 -18.35 -1.8M-18.35,1.1Q-15.1 5.45 -9.6 5.35Q-15.1 9.55 -18.35 1.1M-26.7,2.2L-27.25 2.25Q-28.1 2.4 -28.55 1.2L-28.9 0.35Q-27.7 0.9 -26.7 2.2" />

                </g>
                <g id="Espina">
                    <linearGradient id="LinearGradID_2" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" gradientTransform="matrix(0.0229492, 0, 0, -0.0152893, 0, 0.05)" spreadMethod="pad" x1="-819.2" y1="0" x2="819.2" y2="0">
                        <stop offset="0" style="stop-color: ${palette.main}; stop-opacity: 1" />
                        <stop offset="1" style="stop-color: ${palette.dark}; stop-opacity: 1" />
                    </linearGradient>
                    <path style="fill: url(#LinearGradID_2)" d="M-18.8,0Q-17.85 -5.7 -12.3 -9.6Q-11.2 -5.35 -6.5 -8.25L-6.45 -8.2L-6.2 -8.3Q1.25 -16.25 6.65 -12.4Q0.05 -12.55 0 -5.95Q2.7 -2.4 7.75 -4.1Q18 -1.45 18.8 0L-18.8 0" />

57.15Q-63.75 86.35 -17 98.3Q-24.05 95.95 -30.6 92.75L-43.65 85.9Q-49.9 82.35 -55.45 77.6Q-60.8 73 -64.8 67.15Q-68.9 61.25 -71.85 54.85Q-74.6 49.1 -76.35 42.95L-70.55 43.6L-67 43.6Q-62.95 52.1 -60.5 55.4L-56.8 60.35Q-54.55 63.35 -48.8 66.9Q-43.05 70.4 -36.5 73Q-29.85 75.65 -22.85 77.15Q-16.2 78.55 -9.45 79.15Q-2.2 79.8 5.1 79.35Q-45.9 75.55 -60.6 50.3L-64.7 43.4L-56.05 41.95Q-53.85 45.65 -50.25 50Q-32.2 72.1 21 57.85Q-34.85 68.4 -51.15 44.85L-53.7 41.2L-45.35 38.35L-42.05 40.95Q-17.75 61.45 29.75 36.85Q19.85 40.9 12.75 42.9Q5.95 44.75 -1 46Q-8.35 47.35 -16.2 46.35Q-24.05 45.3 -31.5 43.3Q-38.9 41.25 -42.55 37.05L-34.25 34.05Q-24.55 30.7 -16.75 24.5Q-12.1 20.75 -8.6 15.6" />
                </g>
            </defs>
            <g id="screen" />
        `;

        this.screen = this.svg.querySelector('#screen');
        document.body.appendChild(this.svg);

        window.addEventListener("pointermove", this.onPointerMove);

        this.elems = [];
        this.rad = 0;
        this.frm = Math.random();

        const spawnOffset = 400; 
        const spawnSide = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4); 
        let spawnX, spawnY;
        
        /* ВОЗВРАЩЕНО: Координаты спавна внутри Документа (учитывают скролл) */
        switch(spawnSide) {
            case 0: spawnX = window.scrollX + Math.random() * window.innerWidth; spawnY = window.scrollY - spawnOffset; break;
            case 1: spawnX = window.scrollX + window.innerWidth + spawnOffset; spawnY = window.scrollY + Math.random() * window.innerHeight; break;
            case 2: spawnX = window.scrollX + Math.random() * window.innerWidth; spawnY = window.scrollY + window.innerHeight + spawnOffset; break;
            case 3: spawnX = window.scrollX - spawnOffset; spawnY = window.scrollY + Math.random() * window.innerHeight; break;
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) this.elems[i] = { use: null, x: spawnX, y: spawnY };

        const prepend = (useId, i) => {
            const elem = document.createElementNS(xmlns, "use");
            this.elems[i].use = elem;
            elem.setAttributeNS(xlinkns, "xlink:href", "#" + useId);
            this.screen.prepend(elem);
        };

        for (let i = 1; i < N; i++) {
            if (i === 1) prepend("Cabeza", i);
            else if (wingIndices.has(i)) prepend("Aletas", i);
            else prepend("Espina", i);
        }

        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        console.log(`🐉 [DragonEngine] Запущен. Цвет: ${palette.name}. Лимит FPS: 60`);
        this.loop();
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.rafId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.rafId);
            this.rafId = null;
        }
        window.removeEventListener("pointermove", this.onPointerMove);
        if (this.svg) {
            this.svg.remove();
            this.svg = null;
        }
        console.log('🐉 [DragonEngine] Деактивирован');
    }

    onPointerMove(e) {
        /* ВОЗВРАЩЕНО: Цель дракона живет внутри документа (учитывает скролл) */
        this.pointer.x = e.clientX + window.scrollX; 
        this.pointer.y = e.clientY + window.scrollY; 
        this.rad = 0;
    }

    loop() {
        this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.loop());
        
        const now = performance.now();
        const elapsed = now - this.lastTime;

        if (elapsed < this.fpsInterval) return;
        this.lastTime = now - (elapsed % this.fpsInterval);
        
        try {
            const N = this.elems.length;
            let e = this.elems[0];
            
            const ax = (Math.cos(3 * this.frm) * this.rad * window.innerWidth) / window.innerHeight;
            const ay = (Math.sin(4 * this.frm) * this.rad * window.innerHeight) / window.innerWidth;
            
            e.x += (ax + this.pointer.x - e.x) / 10;
            e.y += (ay + this.pointer.y - e.y) / 10;
            
            for (let i = 1; i < N; i++) {
                let e = this.elems[i];
                let ep = this.elems[i - 1];
                const a = Math.atan2(e.y - ep.y, e.x - ep.x);
                e.x += (ep.x - e.x + (Math.cos(a) * (100 - i)) / 5) / 4;
                e.y += (ep.y - e.y + (Math.sin(a) * (100 - i)) / 5) / 4;
                const s = (1 - (i / N)) * 3 + 0.2; 
                
                if (e.use) {
                    /* МАГИЯ ТУТ: Сама физика (e.x, e.y) живет на уровне сайта.
                       Но чтобы нарисовать ее на стекле экрана (fixed SVG),
                       мы просто вычитаем текущую прокрутку (scrollY) */
                    const renderX = ((ep.x + e.x) / 2) - window.scrollX;
                    const renderY = ((ep.y + e.y) / 2) - window.scrollY;
                    e.use.setAttributeNS(null, "transform", `translate(${renderX},${renderY}) rotate(${(180 / Math.PI) * a}) scale(${s},${s})`);
                }
            }
            if (this.rad < Math.min(this.pointer.x, this.pointer.y) - 20) this.rad++;
            this.frm += 0.003;
            
            if (this.rad > 60) {
                /* ВОЗВРАЩЕНО: Блуждание тоже учитывает прокрутку */
                const centerX = (window.innerWidth / 2) + window.scrollX;
                const centerY = (window.innerHeight / 2) + window.scrollY;
                this.pointer.x += (centerX - this.pointer.x) * 0.05;
                this.pointer.y += (centerY - this.pointer.y) * 0.05;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("🐉 [DragonEngine] Ошибка в физическом цикле:", error);
            this.stop();
        }
    }
}

/* js/event-bus.js */

/**
 * Глобальная шина событий (Event Bus).
 * Обеспечивает слабую связанность компонентов через паттерн Pub/Sub.
 */
class EventBus {
    constructor() {
        this.listeners = {};
        console.log('📡 [EventBus] Инициализирован');
    }

    /**
     * Подписаться на событие
     * @param {string} event - Название события
     * @param {function} callback - Функция-обработчик
     */
    on(event, callback) {
        if (!this.listeners[event]) {
            this.listeners[event] = [];
        }
        this.listeners[event].push(callback);
    }

    /**
     * Отписаться от события
     * @param {string} event - Название события
     * @param {function} callback - Функция-обработчик
     */
    off(event, callback) {
        if (!this.listeners[event]) return;
        this.listeners[event] = this.listeners[event].filter(cb => cb !== callback);
    }

    /**
     * Вызвать событие и передать данные всем подписчикам
     * @param {string} event - Название события
     * @param {any} payload - Данные для передачи
     */
    emit(event, payload = null) {
        if (!this.listeners[event]) return;
        this.listeners[event].forEach(callback => {
            try {
                callback(payload);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error(`🚨 [EventBus] Ошибка в обработчике события "${event}":`, error);
            }
        });
    }
}

// Экспортируем как Singleton (единый экземпляр для всего приложения)
export default new EventBus();

/* js/FXController.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';
import { ReptileEngine } from './reptile-engine.js';
import { DragonEngine } from './dragon-engine.js';
import { CometEngine } from './comets.js';
import { MatrixEngine } from './matrix-engine.js';

export class FXController {
    constructor() {
        this.engines = {
            reptile: new ReptileEngine(),
            dragon: new DragonEngine(),
            comet: new CometEngine(),
            matrix: new MatrixEngine()
        };
    }

    init() {
        this.registerEventHandlers();
        this.engines.comet.startIdle();
        console.log('✨ [FXController] Графические движки инициализированы');
    }

    registerEventHandlers() {
        EventBus.on('CMD_CLEAR', () => {
            this.engines.reptile.stop(); 
            this.engines.dragon.stop(); 
            this.engines.comet.stopShower();
            this.engines.matrix.stop();
        });
        
        EventBus.on('CMD_LIZARD', () => { 
            this.engines.dragon.stop(); 
            this.engines.reptile.start(); 
        });
        
        EventBus.on('CMD_DRAGON', () => { 
            this.engines.reptile.stop(); 
            this.engines.dragon.start(); 
        });
        
        EventBus.on('CMD_COMET', () => this.engines.comet.triggerShower());
        
        EventBus.on('UI_CLICK_CLEAR', () => { 
            this.engines.reptile.stop(); 
            this.engines.dragon.stop(); 
        });
        
        EventBus.on('CMD_GOD', () => AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_TOGGLE_GODMODE' }));
        EventBus.on('CMD_GODMODE', () => AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'FX_TOGGLE_GODMODE' }));

        // Синхронизация состояний из AppStore
        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            
            // 1. Управление паузой
            if (state.fx.isPaused !== prevState?.fx?.isPaused) {
                if (state.fx.isPaused) {
                    this.pauseAll();
                } else {
                    this.resumeAll(state.fx.mode);
                }
            }

            // 2. Управление режимами скролла и фонов (Если не на паузе)
            if (!state.fx.isPaused && state.fx.mode !== prevState?.fx?.mode) {
                this.applyFxMode(state.fx.mode, state.fx.isGodMode);
            }

            // 3. Управление режимом бога
            if (state.fx.isGodMode !== prevState?.fx?.isGodMode) {
                if (state.fx.isGodMode) {
                    if (!state.fx.isPaused) this.engines.matrix.start();
                    if (this.engines.matrix.curtain) this.engines.matrix.curtain.style.opacity = '0';
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'PROTOCOL', action: 'GOD_MODE', value: 'ACTIVATED', color: '#ffd700' });
                } else {
                    this.engines.matrix.stop();
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'PROTOCOL', action: 'GOD_MODE', value: 'DEACTIVATED', color: '#888' });
                }
                this.engines.matrix.isGodMode = state.fx.isGodMode;
            }
        });
    }

    pauseAll() {
        this.engines.reptile.stop();
        this.engines.dragon.stop();
        this.engines.comet.stopIdle();
        this.engines.comet.stopShower();
        this.engines.matrix.stop();
    }

    resumeAll(mode) {
        // Возобновляем только дефолтные фоны. Драконы вызываются вручную, поэтому не восстанавливаем.
        this.applyFxMode(mode, AppStore.getState().fx.isGodMode);
    }

    applyFxMode(mode, isGodMode) {
        document.body.classList.remove('state-no-comets', 'state-no-stars');
            
        if (mode === 0) {
            if (isGodMode) this.engines.matrix.start();
            this.engines.comet.startIdle();
        }
        else if (mode === 1) {
            document.body.classList.add('state-no-comets');
        }
        else if (mode === 2) {
            document.body.classList.add('state-no-stars');
        }
        else if (mode === 3) {
            document.body.classList.add('state-no-comets', 'state-no-stars');
            this.engines.matrix.stop();
            this.engines.comet.stopIdle();
        }
    }
}

/* js/hero-controller.js */
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { Typewriter } from './utils/Typewriter.js';

export class HeroController {
    constructor() {
        this.els = {
            heroSection: document.querySelector('.hero-section-hud'),
            dataTicker: document.getElementById('data-ticker'),
            statusOuter: document.querySelector('.main-display-status'),
            statusInner: document.getElementById('live-status-container')
        };
        
        if (this.els.statusInner) {
            this.els.statusText = this.els.statusInner.querySelector('.status-text');
            this.els.statusDot = this.els.statusInner.querySelector('.status-indicator');
        }
        
        this.messages = AppConfig.texts.heroTicker || ["СИСТЕМЫ В НОРМЕ."];
        this.typewriter = null;

        this.init();
    }

    init() {
        if (!this.els.heroSection) return;
        
        if (this.els.dataTicker) {
            this.typewriter = new Typewriter(this.els.dataTicker);
            setTimeout(() => this.typewriter.loop(() => this.messages), 1000);
        }
        
        EventBus.on('TWITCH_DATA_UPDATED', (data) => this.updateDynamicData(data));
    }

    updateDynamicData(data) {
        const formatNum = (num) => num >= 1000 ? (num / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'K' : num.toString();

        let newMessages = [
            "АНАЛИЗ СТАТИСТИКИ КАНАЛА...",
            `АГЕНТОВ В СЕТИ: ${formatNum(data.followers)}`,
            "ЛОЯЛЬНОСТЬ АУДИТОРИИ: 95%"
        ];

        if (data.isLive) {
            newMessages.push(`ПРЯМАЯ ТРАНСЛЯЦИЯ: ${data.game.toUpperCase()}`);
            newMessages.push(`ЗРИТЕЛЕЙ В ЭФИРЕ: ${formatNum(data.viewers)}`);
        } else {
            newMessages.push("СИСТЕМЫ В НОРМЕ.");
            newMessages.push("ОЖИДАНИЕ СИГНАЛА СТРИМЕРА.");
        }

        this.messages = newMessages;

        if (this.els.statusText && this.els.statusDot && this.els.statusOuter) {
            const isLive = data.isLive;
            // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: была опечатка в имени CSS-переменной
            const color = isLive ? '#ff4444' : 'var(--color-neon-pink)';
            const text = isLive ? 'В ЭФИРЕ' : 'СИСТЕМА АКТИВНА';
            
            this.els.statusText.textContent = text;
            this.els.statusText.style.color = color;
            
            this.els.statusDot.style.background = color;
            this.els.statusDot.style.boxShadow = `0 0 10px ${color}`;
            
            this.els.statusOuter.style.borderColor = color;
            this.els.statusOuter.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${isLive ? 'rgba(255, 68, 68, 0.3)' : 'rgba(255, 45, 149, 0.2)'}`;
        }
    }
}

/* js/hub-controller.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';

export class HubController {
    constructor() {
        this.els = {
            playerContainer: document.getElementById('hub-player-container'),
            chatContainer: document.getElementById('hub-chat-container'),
            networkList: document.getElementById('hub-network-list')
        };

        if (this.els.playerContainer) this.init();
    }

    init() {
        console.log(`🛰️ [HubController] Инициализация Network Hub...`);
        this.embedTwitch();
        this.fetchNetworkStatus();
        setInterval(() => this.fetchNetworkStatus(), 120000);
    }

    embedTwitch() {
        let domain = window.location.hostname || "localhost";
        // Twitch не принимает пустой домен или 127.0.0.1 в качестве parent
        if (domain === "127.0.0.1" || domain === "") domain = "localhost";

        if (this.els.playerContainer) {
            // Добавлен autoplay=false чтобы плеер не падал при блокировках браузером
            this.els.playerContainer.innerHTML = `
                <iframe 
                    src="https://player.twitch.tv/?channel=${AppConfig.twitch.channel}&parent=${domain}&muted=true&autoplay=false" 
                    allowfullscreen="true"
                    class="twitch-iframe"
                    sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-presentation">
                </iframe>
            `;
        }

        if (this.els.chatContainer) {
            this.els.chatContainer.innerHTML = `
                <iframe 
                    src="https://www.twitch.tv/embed/${AppConfig.twitch.channel}/chat?darkpopout&parent=${domain}"
                    class="twitch-iframe"
                    sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox">
                </iframe>
            `;
        }
    }

    async fetchNetworkStatus() {
        if (!this.els.networkList) return;
        
        try {
            const response = await fetch(`${AppConfig.api.ivrBaseUrl}?login=${AppConfig.twitch.allies}&t=${Date.now()}`);
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Network Error');
            
            const users = await response.json();
            this.renderNetwork(users);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("❌ [HubController] Ошибка радара:", error);
            this.els.networkList.innerHTML = `<div class="network-error">ОШИБКА ПОДКЛЮЧЕНИЯ К СЕТИ</div>`;
        }
    }

    renderNetwork(users) {
        if (!users || users.length === 0) return;
        
        this.els.networkList.innerHTML = users.map(user => {
            const isLive = user.stream !== null;
            const gameName = isLive && user.stream.game ? user.stream.game.displayName : (user.lastBroadcast?.game?.displayName || 'Just Chatting');
            
            return `
                <a href="https://www.twitch.tv/${user.login}" target="_blank" class="ally-net-card ${isLive ? 'is-live' : ''}">
                    <img src="${user.logo}" class="ally-net-avatar" alt="${user.displayName}">
                    <div class="ally-net-info">
                        <div class="ally-net-name">${user.displayName}</div>
                        <div class="ally-net-game">${gameName}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ally-net-status">${isLive ? 'LIVE' : 'ОФЛАЙН'}</div>
                </a>
            `;
        }).join('');
    }
}
