/* js/library-controller.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';
import { CardFactory } from './archive/CardFactory.js';
import { LazyLoader } from './archive/LazyLoader.js';
import { SearchEngine } from './services/SearchEngine.js';
import { optimizeImageUrl } from './utils.js';
import { STATUS_MAP } from './media-store.js';

export class LibraryController {
    constructor() {
        this.container = document.getElementById('route-library');
        if (!this.container) return;

        this.els = {
            backBtn: document.getElementById('btn-library-back'),
            gridMain: document.getElementById('library-grid-main'),
            
            sortBtns: document.querySelectorAll('#library-sort-switcher .sort-btn'),
            typeBtns: document.querySelectorAll('#library-type-switcher .switcher-btn'),
            typeSwitcher: document.getElementById('library-type-switcher'),
            statusFilters: document.getElementById('library-filters-status'),
            btnDetailed: document.getElementById('lib-grid-detailed'),
            btnCompact: document.getElementById('lib-grid-compact'),
            counter: document.querySelector('.lib-counter-val'),
            
            searchModule: document.getElementById('lib-search-module'),
            searchInput: document.getElementById('lib-search-input'),
            searchIcon: document.getElementById('lib-search-icon'),
            searchLoading: document.getElementById('lib-search-loading'),
            searchHotkey: document.getElementById('lib-search-hotkey'),
            suggestionsBox: document.getElementById('lib-search-suggestions')
        };

        this.gridMode = 'detailed'; 
        this.localSearchQuery = '';
        
        this.searchDebounce = null;
        this.currentSearchMatches = [];
        this.selectedIndex = -1;

        this.factory = new CardFactory(() => this.gridMode);
        this.lazyLoader = new LazyLoader(() => this.gridMode);

        this.bindEvents();
    }

    mount() {
        console.log('📚 [Library] Компонент смонтирован');
        this.localSearchQuery = AppStore.getState().media.searchQuery || '';
        this.els.searchInput.value = this.localSearchQuery;
        this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        this.syncUIWithState();
        this.renderGrid();
    }

    unmount() {
        console.log('📚 [Library] Компонент размонтирован');
    }

    bindEvents() {
        this.els.backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            const type = AppStore.getState().media.type || 'games';
            AppRouter.navigate('/archive', { type: type });
        });

        this.els.sortBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                const state = AppStore.getState().media;
                const newSortDir = state.sort === btn.dataset.sort 
                    ? (state.sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc') 
                    : (btn.dataset.sort === 'rating' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
                
                AppRouter.updateQuery({ sort: btn.dataset.sort, sortDir: newSortDir });
            });
        });

        this.els.typeBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const type = btn.dataset.type;
                if (AppStore.getState().media.type !== type) {
                    EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                    const currentQuery = { ...AppStore.getState().route.query };
                    delete currentQuery.filters; 
                    AppRouter.navigate('/library', { type }, currentQuery);
                }
            });
        });

        const updateGridMode = (mode) => {
            if (this.gridMode === mode) return;
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            this.gridMode = mode;
            this.els.btnDetailed.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'detailed');
            this.els.btnCompact.classList.toggle('active', mode === 'compact');
            
            if (mode === 'compact') this.els.gridMain.classList.add('compact-mode');
            else this.els.gridMain.classList.remove('compact-mode');
            
            this.els.gridMain.style.opacity = '0';
            setTimeout(() => { this.renderGrid(); this.els.gridMain.style.opacity = '1'; }, 200);
        };

        this.els.btnDetailed.addEventListener('click', () => updateGridMode('detailed'));
        this.els.btnCompact.addEventListener('click', () => updateGridMode('compact'));

        this.els.gridMain.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const card = e.target.closest('.archive-card-container');
            if (!card || card.classList.contains('archive-divider-row')) return;
            const state = AppStore.getState();
            if (!state.data.isMediaLoaded) return;
            const item = state.data.media.find(i => i.id === card.dataset.id);
            if (item) {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'expand');
                // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Добавляем метку откуда пришли
                AppRouter.navigate('/item', { id: item.id }, { from: 'library' });
            }
        });

        this.els.searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.handleSearchInput(e.target.value));
        this.els.searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleSearchKeyDown(e));

        this.els.searchIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!this.els.searchModule.contains(e.target)) this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        });

        this.els.suggestionsBox.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const itemEl = e.target.closest('.suggestion-item');
            if (itemEl) {
                const index = parseInt(itemEl.dataset.index);
                if (!isNaN(index) && this.currentSearchMatches[index]) {
                    this.applySearchSelection(this.currentSearchMatches[index]);
                }
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
                if (AppStore.getState().route.path.startsWith('/library')) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    this.els.searchInput.focus();
                }
            }
        });

        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            const curr = state.media;
            const prev = prevState?.media;
            
            if (state.route.path.startsWith('/library')) {
                if (
                    curr.type !== prev?.type || 
                    JSON.stringify(curr.filters) !== JSON.stringify(prev?.filters) ||
                    curr.sort !== prev?.sort ||
                    curr.sortDirection !== prev?.sortDirection
                ) {
                    this.syncUIWithState();
                }
                
                if (
                    JSON.stringify(curr.filters) !== JSON.stringify(prev?.filters) ||
                    curr.sort !== prev?.sort ||
                    curr.sortDirection !== prev?.sortDirection ||
                    curr.searchQuery !== prev?.searchQuery
                ) {
                    if (curr.searchQuery !== undefined) {
                        this.localSearchQuery = curr.searchQuery;
                        this.els.searchInput.value = this.localSearchQuery;
                    }
                    this.renderGrid();
                }
            }
        });

        EventBus.on('MEDIA_STORE_LOADING', () => {
            if (AppStore.getState().route.path.startsWith('/library')) {
                this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%; grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align:center; padding:100px; color:var(--color-neon-pink); font-size:1.5rem;"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> ЗАГРУЗКА БАЗЫ ДАННЫХ...</div>';
                this.els.counter.textContent = '0';
            }
        });

        EventBus.on('MEDIA_STORE_LOADED', () => {
            if (AppStore.getState().route.path.startsWith('/library')) {
                this.renderGrid();
            }
        });

        this.els.gridMain.addEventListener('mouseenter', (e) => {
            const card = e.target.closest('.archive-card-container');
            if (!card || this.gridMode === 'compact') return;
            card.querySelectorAll('.layer-back[data-lazy-bg], .layer-back-deep[data-lazy-bg]').forEach(layer => {
                const bgUrl = layer.dataset.lazyBg;
                if (bgUrl && !layer.style.backgroundImage) {
                    this.lazyLoader._safeImageLoad(bgUrl, card.classList.contains('is-youtube'), layer, null, null, layer);
                    layer.removeAttribute('data-lazy-bg');
                }
            });
        }, true);
    }

    executeSearch(query) {
        this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        AppRouter.updateQuery({ search: query.trim() ? query : null });
    }

    handleSearchInput(text) {
        const query = text.toLowerCase().trim();
        if (this.els.searchHotkey) this.els.searchHotkey.style.display = query.length > 0 ? 'none' : 'flex';

        clearTimeout(this.searchDebounce);
        
        if (query.length < 1) {
            this.toggleSuggestions(false);
            this.toggleLoading(false);
            if (this.localSearchQuery !== '') {
                this.executeSearch('');
            }
            return;
        }

        this.toggleLoading(true);
        
        this.searchDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
            const state = AppStore.getState();
            if (!state.data.isMediaLoaded) {
                this.toggleLoading(false);
                return;
            }

            let flattenedList = [];
            state.data.media.forEach(item => {
                if (item.type !== state.media.type) return; 
                if (item.format === 'youtube') return; 
                
                if (item.format === 'collection' && item.items) {
                    item.items.forEach(sub => flattenedList.push({ ...sub, status: sub.status || item.status }));
                } else {
                    flattenedList.push(item);
                }
            });

            const uniqueMap = new Map();
            flattenedList.forEach(item => uniqueMap.set(item.id, item));
            flattenedList = Array.from(uniqueMap.values());

            let matches = flattenedList.map(item => ({
                ...item,
                score: SearchEngine.scoreItemForSuggestions(item.title, query)
            })).filter(item => item.score > 15);

            matches = matches.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 5);
            this.currentSearchMatches = matches;
            
            this.renderSuggestions(matches);
            this.toggleLoading(false);
        }, 300);
    }

    handleSearchKeyDown(e) {
        const isDropdownOpen = this.els.searchModule.classList.contains('suggestions-open');
        
        if (!isDropdownOpen || this.currentSearchMatches.length === 0) {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
            }
            return;
        }

        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.selectedIndex = (this.selectedIndex + 1) % this.currentSearchMatches.length;
            this.updateSelectionUI();
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.selectedIndex = (this.selectedIndex - 1 + this.currentSearchMatches.length) % this.currentSearchMatches.length;
            this.updateSelectionUI();
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.selectedIndex >= 0 && this.currentSearchMatches[this.selectedIndex]) {
                this.applySearchSelection(this.currentSearchMatches[this.selectedIndex]);
            } else {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
            }
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        }
    }

    updateSelectionUI() {
        const items = this.els.suggestionsBox.querySelectorAll('.suggestion-item');
        items.forEach((item, idx) => {
            if (idx === this.selectedIndex) {
                item.classList.add('keyboard-focus');
                item.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
            } else {
                item.classList.remove('keyboard-focus');
            }
        });
    }

    applySearchSelection(item) {
        EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
        this.els.searchInput.value = item.title;
        if (this.els.searchHotkey) this.els.searchHotkey.style.display = 'none';
        
        this.executeSearch(item.title);
    }

    toggleSuggestions(isOpen) {
        this.els.suggestionsBox.classList.toggle('active', isOpen);
        this.els.searchModule.classList.toggle('suggestions-open', isOpen);
        if (!isOpen) this.selectedIndex = -1; 
    }

    toggleLoading(isLoading) {
        if (!this.els.searchIcon || !this.els.searchLoading) return;
        this.els.searchIcon.style.display = isLoading ? 'none' : 'flex';
        this.els.searchLoading.style.display = isLoading ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }

    renderSuggestions(matches) {
        if (matches.length === 0) {
            this.els.suggestionsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="suggestion-empty"><i class="fas fa-search"></i>Совпадений не найдено</div>';
        } else {
            this.els.suggestionsBox.innerHTML = matches.map((item, idx) => {
                const img = item.image || (item.images ? item.images[0] : '') || 'https://via.placeholder.com/45x65?text=NO+IMG';
                return `
                    <div class="suggestion-item" data-index="${idx}">
                        <div class="sugg-left">
                            <div class="sugg-thumb-wrapper">
                                <img src="${optimizeImageUrl(img, 100, 70)}" class="sugg-thumb">
                            </div>
                            <span class="sugg-title">${item.title}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }
        this.toggleSuggestions(true);
    }

    syncUIWithState() {
        const state = AppStore.getState().media;
        
        if (this.els.typeSwitcher) {
            this.els.typeSwitcher.classList.toggle('movies-active', state.type === 'movies');
        }
        this.els.typeBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.type === state.type));
        
        this.els.sortBtns.forEach(btn => {
            const icon = btn.querySelector('.sort-icon');
            if (btn.dataset.sort === state.sort) {
                if (!btn.classList.contains('active')) btn.classList.add('active');
                
                let newIconClass = btn.dataset.sort === 'name' 
                    ? (state.sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'fas fa-sort-alpha-down' : 'fas fa-sort-alpha-up')
                    : (state.sortDirection === 'desc' ? 'fas fa-sort-numeric-down-alt' : 'fas fa-sort-numeric-up-alt');
                
                if (icon && !icon.className.includes(newIconClass)) {
                    icon.className = `${newIconClass} sort-icon`;
                }
            } else {
                btn.classList.remove('active');
                if (icon) icon.className = btn.dataset.sort === 'name' ? 'fas fa-sort-alpha-down sort-icon' : 'fas fa-star sort-icon';
            }
        });

        let statusesOrder = state.type === 'games' 
            ? ['completed', 'playing', 'on-hold', 'dropped']
            : ['watched', 'watching', 'on-hold', 'dropped'];
        
        const isActive = (val) => state.filters.includes(val) ? 'active' : '';

        this.els.statusFilters.innerHTML = statusesOrder.map(s => `
            <div class="filter-chip is-status status-${s} ${isActive(s)}" data-filter="${s}">
                ${STATUS_MAP[s] || s}
            </div>
        `).join('');

        this.els.statusFilters.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(chip => {
            chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                const val = chip.dataset.filter;
                const currentFilters = AppStore.getState().media.filters;
                const newFilters = currentFilters.includes(val) ? [] : [val];
                AppRouter.updateQuery({ filters: newFilters.length ? newFilters.join(',') : null });
            });
        });
    }

    renderGrid() {
        const storeData = AppStore.getState().data;
        if (!storeData.isMediaLoaded) {
            this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%; grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align:center; padding:100px; color:var(--color-neon-pink); font-size:1.5rem;"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> ЗАГРУЗКА БАЗЫ ДАННЫХ...</div>';
            this.els.counter.textContent = '0';
            return;
        }

        const state = AppStore.getState().media;
        const isAllSelected = state.filters.length === 0;
        
        let allFilteredData = storeData.media.filter(item => {
            if (item.type !== state.type) return false;
            if (item.format === 'youtube') return false; 
            if (!isAllSelected && !state.filters.includes(item.status)) return false;

            if (this.localSearchQuery.length > 0) {
                let textToSearch = `${item.title || ''} ${item.description || ''}`.toLowerCase();
                if (item.format === 'collection' && item.items) {
                    item.items.forEach(sub => { textToSearch += ` ${sub.title || ''} ${sub.description || ''}`; });
                }
                const score = SearchEngine.scoreItemForGrid(textToSearch, item.title, this.localSearchQuery);
                if (score === 0) return false;
                item._matchScore = score;
            }

            return true;
        });

        let mainData = allFilteredData.filter(i => i.status !== 'suggested');
        let suggData = allFilteredData.filter(i => i.status === 'suggested');

        const dir = state.sortDirection === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        const sortFn = (a, b) => {
            if (this.localSearchQuery.length > 0 && Math.abs((a._matchScore||0) - (b._matchScore||0)) > 0.1) {
                return (b._matchScore||0) - (a._matchScore||0);
            }
            if (state.sort === 'rating') {
                return ((a.rating || 0) - (b.rating || 0)) * dir;
            }
            return (a.title || "").localeCompare(b.title || "") * dir;
        };

        mainData.sort(sortFn);
        suggData.sort(sortFn);

        this.els.counter.textContent = mainData.length + suggData.length;

        if (mainData.length === 0 && suggData.length === 0) {
            this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%; grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align:center; padding:100px; color:#666; font-family:\'Exo 2\'; font-size:1.2rem;">ПО ВАШИМ ПАРАМЕТРАМ НИЧЕГО НЕ НАЙДЕНО</div>';
            return;
        }

        let htmlBuf = '';
        let globalIndex = 0;

        mainData.forEach((item) => {
            htmlBuf += this.factory.createCardHTML(item, (globalIndex % 15) * 20);
            globalIndex++;
        });

        if (suggData.length > 0) {
            htmlBuf += this.factory.createCardHTML({ isDivider: true, title: 'ПРЕДЛОЖЕНИЯ АГЕНТОВ' }, 0);
            suggData.forEach((item) => {
                htmlBuf += this.factory.createCardHTML(item, (globalIndex % 15) * 20);
                globalIndex++;
            });
        }
        
        this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = htmlBuf;

        this.els.gridMain.querySelectorAll('.archive-card-container:not(.archive-divider-row):not(.is-observed)').forEach(card => {
            card.classList.add('is-observed');
            this.lazyLoader.observe(card);
        });
    }
}

================================================================================
File: js\matrix-engine.js
================================================================================

/* js/matrix-engine.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';

const CHARS = "アァカサтанамāяāлавагазадабапаиикишичиниҳимиривигиджидзибипиуукусуцунуфумуююлугузубудзупуеекесеТЕНЕХЕМЕРЕВЕГЕЗЕДЕБЕПЕОокосотонохомоёёловогозодобоповуцн0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";

export class MatrixEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.canvas = null;
        this.ctx = null;
        this.curtain = null;
        
        this.drops = [];
        this.activeEasterEggs = {};
        
        this.rafId = null;
        this.lastDrawTime = 0;
        this.fps = 30;
        this.interval = 1000 / this.fps;
        
        this.isGodMode = false;
        
        this.onResize = this.onResize.bind(this);
    }

    start() {
        if (this.canvas) return;

        // 1. Динамическое создание элементов Canvas
        this.canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
        Object.assign(this.canvas.style, {
            position: 'fixed', top: '0', left: '0', width: '100%', height: '100%',
            zIndex: 'var(--z-matrix)', pointerEvents: 'none', opacity: '0.15'
        });
        document.body.appendChild(this.canvas);

        // 2. Динамическое создание Curtain (Шторки)
        this.curtain = document.createElement('div');
        Object.assign(this.curtain.style, {
            position: 'absolute', top: '0', left: '0', width: '100%', height: '100%',
            backgroundColor: '#050508', zIndex: 'calc(var(--z-matrix) + 5)', pointerEvents: 'none',
            transition: 'opacity 2s ease-in-out', opacity: '1'
        });
        document.body.appendChild(this.curtain);

        // 3. Биндинг событий
        window.addEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
        this.onResize();

        // 4. Запуск цикла
        this.lastDrawTime = performance.now();
        this.loop();
        
        console.log('🤖 [MatrixEngine] Запущен');
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.rafId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.rafId);
            this.rafId = null;
        }
        if (this.canvas) {
            this.canvas.remove();
            this.canvas = null;
            this.ctx = null;
        }
        if (this.curtain) {
            this.curtain.remove();
            this.curtain = null;
        }
        window.removeEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
        console.log('🤖 [MatrixEngine] Деактивирован, ресурсы освобождены');
    }

    onResize() {
        if (!this.canvas) return;
        this.canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
        this.canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
        
        const columns = Math.floor(this.canvas.width / 16);
        this.drops = new Array(columns).fill(1);
    }

    loop() {
        if (!this.canvas) return;
        this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.loop());

        const now = performance.now();
        const delta = now - this.lastDrawTime;

        if (delta > this.interval) {
            this.draw();
            this.lastDrawTime = now - (delta % this.interval);
        }
    }

    draw() {
        if (!this.ctx || !this.canvas) return;

        const secretWords = AppConfig.texts.matrixWords || ["MATRIX"];

        this.ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 5, 8, 0.05)';
        this.ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);
        this.ctx.font = '14px monospace';

        for (let i = 0; i < this.drops.length; i++) {
            let text = "";
            
            if (this.activeEasterEggs[i]) {
                const egg = this.activeEasterEggs[i];
                text = egg.word[egg.charIndex]; 
                this.ctx.fillStyle = egg.color; 
                this.activeEasterEggs[i].charIndex++;
                if (this.activeEasterEggs[i].charIndex >= egg.word.length) delete this.activeEasterEggs[i];
            } 
            else if (Math.random() > 0.999) {
                const randomWord = secretWords[Math.floor(Math.random() * secretWords.length)];
                const colors = ['#ff2d95', '#ffffff', '#ffd700']; 
                const randomColor = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];

                this.activeEasterEggs[i] = { word: randomWord, charIndex: 0, color: randomColor };
                text = randomWord[0];
                this.ctx.fillStyle = randomColor;
                this.activeEasterEggs[i].charIndex++;
            }
            else {
                text = CHARS.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * CHARS.length));
                this.ctx.fillStyle = '#0F0'; 
            }

            this.ctx.fillText(text, i * 16, this.drops[i] * 16);

            if (this.drops[i] * 16 > this.canvas.height && Math.random() > 0.975) {
                this.drops[i] = 0;
                if (this.activeEasterEggs[i]) delete this.activeEasterEggs[i];
            }
            this.drops[i]++;
        }
    }

    toggleGodMode() {
        this.isGodMode = !this.isGodMode;
        if (this.isGodMode) {
            this.start();
            if (this.curtain) this.curtain.style.opacity = '0';
        } else {
            this.stop();
        }
        EventBus.emit('STATE_GODMODE_CHANGED', this.isGodMode);
    }
}

================================================================================

/* js/media-item-controller.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';
import { getYouTubeId, extractColorFromImageAsync, optimizeImageUrl } from './utils.js';
import { STATUS_MAP } from './media-store.js';

export class MediaItemController {
    constructor() {
        this.container = document.getElementById('route-media-item');
        if (!this.container) return;

        this.els = {
            page: document.getElementById('media-item-page'),
            layout: document.getElementById('item-layout'),
            backBtn: document.getElementById('btn-item-back'),
            bg: document.getElementById('item-cinematic-bg'),
            statusBadge: document.getElementById('item-status-badge'),
            
            posterWrapper: document.getElementById('item-poster-wrapper'),
            posterDots: document.getElementById('item-poster-dots'),
            
            ratingBox: document.getElementById('item-rating-box'),
            ratingVal: document.getElementById('item-rating-val'),
            segments: document.getElementById('item-segments'),
            
            barcodeContainer: document.getElementById('item-barcode-container'),
            sysId: document.getElementById('item-sys-id'),
            dataType: document.getElementById('item-datatype'),
            
            mainZone: document.getElementById('item-main-zone'),
            title: document.getElementById('item-title-text'),
            googleLink: document.getElementById('item-google-link'),
            desc: document.getElementById('item-desc-text'),
            
            videoZone: document.getElementById('item-video-zone'),
            iframe: document.getElementById('item-iframe'),
            playlistCol: document.getElementById('item-playlist-col'),
            playlistList: document.getElementById('item-playlist-list')
        };

        this.fallbackColor = '#ff2d95';
        this.currentStack = [];
        this.currentImageIndex = 0;
        
        this.bindEvents();
    }

    bindEvents() {
        this.els.backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            
            const state = AppStore.getState();
            const from = state.route.query.from || 'archive';
            const type = state.media.type || 'games';
            
            // Восстанавливаем параметры (поиск, фильтры, сортировка), чтобы они не сбросились в URL
            const mediaState = state.media;
            const restoreQuery = {};
            if (mediaState.searchQuery) restoreQuery.search = mediaState.searchQuery;
            if (mediaState.filters.length > 0) restoreQuery.filters = mediaState.filters.join(',');
            if (mediaState.sort !== 'name') restoreQuery.sort = mediaState.sort;
            if (mediaState.sortDirection !== 'asc') restoreQuery.sortDir = mediaState.sortDirection;
            if (mediaState.gridMode !== 'detailed') restoreQuery.mode = mediaState.gridMode;

            // Умный возврат туда, откуда пришли
            if (from === 'library') {
                AppRouter.navigate('/library', { type }, restoreQuery);
            } else if (from === 'youtube') {
                AppRouter.navigate('/youtube', {}, restoreQuery);
            } else if (from === 'tierlist') {
                AppRouter.navigate('/tierlist', { type });
            } else {
                AppRouter.navigate('/archive', { type }, restoreQuery);
            }
        });
    }

    mount(itemId) {
        const state = AppStore.getState();
        if (!state.data.isMediaLoaded) {
            const unsubscribe = AppStore.subscribe((newState) => {
                if (newState.data.isMediaLoaded) {
                    unsubscribe();
                    this.mount(itemId);
                }
            });
            return;
        }

        const item = state.data.media.find(i => i.id === itemId || i.title === itemId);
        if (!item) {
            console.warn(`[ItemPage] Объект с ID "${itemId}" не найден.`);
            AppRouter.navigate('/archive');
            return;
        }

        // Динамический текст кнопки "НАЗАД" в зависимости от того, откуда пришли
        const from = state.route.query.from || 'archive';
        if (from === 'library') {
            this.els.backBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i> НАЗАД В БИБЛИОТЕКУ';
        } else if (from === 'youtube') {
            this.els.backBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i> НАЗАД В YOUTUBE АРХИВ';
        } else if (from === 'tierlist') {
            this.els.backBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i> НАЗАД В ТИРЛИСТ';
        } else {
            this.els.backBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i> НАЗАД В БАЗУ';
        }

        this.renderPage(item);
    }

    unmount() {
        this.els.iframe.src = ''; 
    }

    async renderPage(item) {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0); 
        
        this.fallbackColor = item.customColor || (item.format === 'youtube' ? '#ff0000' : '#ff2d95');
        
        if (item.format === 'youtube') {
            this.els.page.classList.add('is-cinema');
        } else {
            this.els.page.classList.remove('is-cinema');
        }

        if (item.format === 'collection' && item.items && item.items.length > 0) {
            this.currentStack = [...item.items];
        } else {
            let images = (item.images && item.images.length > 0) ? item.images : [item.image];
            this.currentStack = images.map(img => ({ ...item, isImageOnly: true, overrideImage: img }));
        }

        this.renderPosters3D();
        this.updateDataFields(this.currentStack[0], item.format, item.type, this.fallbackColor);
        this.renderPlayer(this.currentStack[0].videos, item.format, this.currentStack[0].status);
    }

    updateDataFields(currentItem, format, type, initColor) {
        this.applyThemeColor(initColor);

        this.els.mainZone.classList.remove('is-glitching');
        void this.els.mainZone.offsetWidth;
        this.els.mainZone.classList.add('is-glitching');
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            this.els.title.textContent = currentItem.title;
            this.els.googleLink.href = `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(currentItem.title)}`;
            
            if (!currentItem.description || currentItem.description.trim() === '...' || currentItem.description.trim() === '') {
                this.els.desc.style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                this.els.desc.style.display = 'block';
                this.els.desc.textContent = currentItem.description;
            }

            const effectiveStatus = currentItem.status || 'unknown';
            
            if (effectiveStatus === 'suggested') {
                this.els.ratingBox.style.display = 'none';
                this.els.statusBadge.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-user"></i> Предложил: ${currentItem.suggestedBy || 'Агент'}`;
            } else {
                this.els.ratingBox.style.display = 'flex';
                this.els.statusBadge.textContent = STATUS_MAP[effectiveStatus] || effectiveStatus;
                
                let rating = currentItem.rating || 0;
                if (rating > 0) {
                    this.els.ratingVal.textContent = rating.toFixed(1);
                    const rScore = Math.round(rating);
                    this.els.segments.innerHTML = Array.from({length: 5}, (_, i) => 
                        `<div class="modal-segment-line ${i < rScore ? 'filled' : ''}"></div>`
                    ).join('');
                } else {
                    this.els.ratingVal.textContent = "N/A";
                    this.els.segments.innerHTML = `<span style="font-family:monospace;font-size:0.7rem;color:#666;">NO_DATA</span>`;
                }
            }
            
            this.els.sysId.textContent = currentItem.id || "SYS_UNKNOWN";
            this.els.dataType.textContent = type ? type.toUpperCase() : "UNKNOWN";
            
            this.generateBarcode(this.els.sysId.textContent.toUpperCase(), initColor);
            
            const bgImg = currentItem.overrideImage || currentItem.image || (currentItem.videos ? `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${getYouTubeId(currentItem.videos[0])}/hqdefault.jpg` : null);
            if (bgImg) this.els.bg.style.backgroundImage = `url('${optimizeImageUrl(bgImg, 1200, 80)}')`;

        }, 150); 
    }

    renderPosters3D() {
        this.currentImageIndex = 0;
        this.els.posterWrapper.innerHTML = '<div class="modal-poster-glow"></div>';
        this.els.posterDots.innerHTML = '';
        
        this.els.posterWrapper.classList.remove('stack-1', 'stack-2', 'stack-3');
        const stackCount = Math.min(this.currentStack.length, 3);
        this.els.posterWrapper.classList.add(`stack-${stackCount}`);

        this.imgElements = [];

        this.currentStack.forEach((data, index) => {
            const img = document.createElement('img');
            img.crossOrigin = "Anonymous"; 
            img.decoding = "async"; 
            
            img.onload = async () => {
                const neonColor = await extractColorFromImageAsync(img) || data.customColor || this.fallbackColor;
                img.dataset.neonColor = neonColor;
                if (index === this.currentImageIndex) this.applyThemeColor(neonColor);
            };

            img.onerror = () => {
                img.dataset.neonColor = this.fallbackColor;
                img.src = 'https://via.placeholder.com/600x900/1a1a24/ffffff?text=NO+IMAGE'; 
                if (index === this.currentImageIndex) this.applyThemeColor(this.fallbackColor);
            };
            
            let rawSrc = data.overrideImage || data.image || (data.videos ? `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${getYouTubeId(data.videos[0])}/hqdefault.jpg` : 'https://via.placeholder.com/600x900/1a1a24/ffffff?text=NO+IMAGE');
            img.src = optimizeImageUrl(rawSrc, 600, 90); 
            img.className = 'modal-poster-img'; 
            
            this.els.posterWrapper.appendChild(img); 
            this.imgElements.push(img);
            
            if (this.currentStack.length > 1) {
                const dot = document.createElement('div');
                dot.className = 'poster-dot';
                this.els.posterDots.appendChild(dot);
            }
        });

        this.updatePosterClasses();

        if (this.currentStack.length > 1) {
            this.els.posterWrapper.classList.add('is-interactive');
            this.els.posterWrapper.onclick = () => { 
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'hover'); 
                this.currentImageIndex = (this.currentImageIndex + 1) % this.imgElements.length; 
                this.updatePosterClasses(); 
                
                const currentItem = this.currentStack[this.currentImageIndex];
                const activeImg = this.imgElements[this.currentImageIndex];
                const currentExtractedColor = activeImg.dataset.neonColor || this.fallbackColor;
                
                if (!currentItem.isImageOnly) {
                    this.updateDataFields(currentItem, currentItem.format, currentItem.type, currentExtractedColor);
                    this.renderPlayer(currentItem.videos, currentItem.format, currentItem.status);
                } else {
                    this.applyThemeColor(currentExtractedColor);
                    const bgImg = currentItem.overrideImage || currentItem.image;
                    if (bgImg) this.els.bg.style.backgroundImage = `url('${optimizeImageUrl(bgImg, 1200, 80)}')`;
                }
            };
        } else {
            this.els.posterWrapper.classList.remove('is-interactive');
            this.els.posterWrapper.onclick = null;
        }
    }

    updatePosterClasses() {
        this.imgElements.forEach((img, index) => {
            img.classList.remove('is-front', 'is-back', 'is-back-2', 'is-hidden');
            let relIndex = (index - this.currentImageIndex + this.imgElements.length) % this.imgElements.length;
            if (relIndex === 0) { img.classList.add('is-front'); img.style.zIndex = 30; }
            else if (relIndex === 1) { img.classList.add('is-back'); img.style.zIndex = 20; }
            else if (relIndex === 2) { img.classList.add('is-back-2'); img.style.zIndex = 10; }
            else { img.classList.add('is-hidden'); img.style.zIndex = 0; }
        });
        
        if (this.currentStack.length > 1) {
            const dots = this.els.posterDots.querySelectorAll('.poster-dot');
            dots.forEach((dot, index) => { dot.classList.toggle('active', index === this.currentImageIndex); });
        }
    }

    renderPlayer(videos, format, status) {
        if (!videos || videos.length === 0) {
            this.els.videoZone.style.display = 'none';
            this.els.playlistCol.style.display = 'none';
            this.els.iframe.src = '';
            this.els.page.classList.remove('has-playlist');
            return;
        }

        this.els.videoZone.style.display = 'block';
        this.els.playlistList.innerHTML = '';
        let firstValidId = null;

        videos.forEach((url, index) => {
            const vidId = getYouTubeId(url);
            if (!vidId) return;
            if (!firstValidId) firstValidId = vidId;

            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = `item-playlist-card ${index === 0 ? 'active' : ''}`;
            card.innerHTML = `
                <div class="p-thumb">
                    <img src="https://img.youtube.com/vi/${vidId}/hqdefault.jpg" alt="thumbnail">
                    <div class="p-overlay"><i class="fas fa-play play-icon"></i><div class="p-equalizer"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div></div>
                </div>
                <div class="p-info">
                    <div class="p-title">Запись #${index + 1}</div>
                    <div class="p-status">СМОТРЕТЬ</div>
                </div>
            `;

            const titleEl = card.querySelector('.p-title');

            card.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.els.playlistList.querySelectorAll('.item-playlist-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
                card.classList.add('active');
                this.els.iframe.src = `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${vidId}?autoplay=0&rel=0&modestbranding=1`;
                
                if (status === 'suggested') {
                    this.els.title.textContent = titleEl.textContent;
                    this.els.googleLink.href = `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(titleEl.textContent)}`;
                }
            });

            this.els.playlistList.appendChild(card);

            fetch(`https://noembed.com/embed?url=${url}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
                if (d.title) {
                    titleEl.textContent = d.title;
                    if (card.classList.contains('active') && status === 'suggested') {
                        this.els.title.textContent = d.title;
                        this.els.googleLink.href = `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(d.title)}`;
                    }
                }
            }).catch(() => {});
        });

        if (videos.length > 1) {
            this.els.playlistCol.style.display = 'flex';
            this.els.page.classList.add('has-playlist');
        } else {
            this.els.playlistCol.style.display = 'none';
            this.els.page.classList.remove('has-playlist');
        }

        if (firstValidId) {
            this.els.iframe.src = `https://www.youtube.com/embed/${firstValidId}?autoplay=0&rel=0&modestbranding=1`;
        }
    }

    applyThemeColor(color) {
        this.els.page.style.setProperty('--item-color', color);
        
        this.els.ratingVal.style.color = color;
        this.els.segments.querySelectorAll('.filled').forEach(s => s.style.color = color);
    }

    generateBarcode(textId, color) {
        const linesContainer = this.els.barcodeContainer.querySelector('.barcode-lines');
        if (!linesContainer) return;
        linesContainer.innerHTML = '';
        try {
            if (window.JsBarcode) {
                const svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
                svg.style.height = "100%";
                svg.style.filter = `drop-shadow(0 0 3px ${color})`;
                linesContainer.appendChild(svg);
                window.JsBarcode(svg, textId, { format: "CODE39", displayValue: false, background: "transparent", lineColor: color, margin: 0, width: 2, height: 40 });
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }
}

/* js/media-manager.js */
import { MediaStore } from './media-store.js';
import { CardFactory } from './archive/CardFactory.js';
import { LazyLoader } from './archive/LazyLoader.js';
import { SearchController } from './archive/SearchController.js';
import { FilterController } from './archive/FilterController.js';
import { GridManager } from './archive/GridManager.js';

export async function initMediaArchive() {
    try {
        console.log('📂 [MediaArchive] Инициализация модульной архитектуры...');
        
        const store = new MediaStore();
        
        // Лямбда для получения текущего режима сетки (detailed/compact)
        const getGridMode = () => gridManager ? gridManager.gridMode : 'detailed';
        
        const factory = new CardFactory(getGridMode);
        const lazyLoader = new LazyLoader(getGridMode);
        
        // GridManager теперь инициализирует внутри себя новый ArchivePagination
        const gridManager = new GridManager(store, factory, lazyLoader);
        
        new SearchController();
        new FilterController(gridManager);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("❌ [MediaArchive] Критическая ошибка при инициализации:", error);
    }
}

================================================================================
File: js\media-store.js

/* js/media-store.js */
import { loadData } from './api.js';
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { SearchEngine } from './services/SearchEngine.js';
import { getYouTubeId } from './utils.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';

export const STATUS_MAP = {
    'completed': 'ПРОЙДЕНО', 'watched': 'ПОСМОТРЕНО',
    'playing': 'В ПРОЦЕССЕ', 'watching': 'СМОТРИМ',
    'dropped': 'БРОШЕНО', 'on-hold': 'ПОД ВОПРОСОМ',
    'suggested': 'ПРЕДЛОЖКА'
};

export class MediaStore {
    constructor() {
        this.dataMain = [];
        this.dataSuggestions = [];
        
        this.filteredMain = [];
        this.filteredSuggestions = [];
        this.combinedData = [];
        
        this.filteredMainStandard = [];
        this.filteredSuggStandard = [];
        this.filteredYoutube = []; // Оставляем массив пустым для обратной совместимости (чтобы ничего не крашнулось)
        
        this.currentLoadId = 0; 
        this.activeLoadedType = null; 
        
        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            const m1 = state.media;
            const m2 = prevState?.media || {};
            
            if (m1.type !== m2.type && m1.type) {
                this.loadType(m1.type);
            } 
            else if (
                m1.searchQuery !== m2.searchQuery ||
                JSON.stringify(m1.filters) !== JSON.stringify(m2.filters) ||
                m1.sort !== m2.sort ||
                m1.sortDirection !== m2.sortDirection
            ) {
                this.processData();
                EventBus.emit('MEDIA_STORE_UPDATED');
            }
        });

        const initialType = AppStore.getState().media.type || 'games';
        this.loadType(initialType);
    }

    async loadType(type) {
        if (this.activeLoadedType === type) return;
        this.activeLoadedType = type;

        this.currentLoadId++;
        const loadId = this.currentLoadId;
        
        EventBus.emit('MEDIA_STORE_LOADING');
        
        const endpoint = type === 'games' ? 'games.json' : 'movies.json';
        
        try {
            const rawMain = await loadData(endpoint, []);
            
            const [sugGames, sugMovies, sugYt] = await Promise.all([
                loadData('suggestions-games.json', []),
                loadData('suggestions-movies.json', []),
                loadData('suggestions-youtube.json', [])
            ]);
            
            if (this.currentLoadId !== loadId) {
                return; 
            }
            
            const injectMeta = (array, defaultType, defaultFormat, idPrefix) => {
                return (Array.isArray(array) ? array : []).map((item, index) => ({
                    ...item,
                    id: item.id || `${idPrefix}-${Date.now()}-${index}`,
                    type: item.type || defaultType,
                    format: item.format || (item.items ? 'collection' : defaultFormat)
                }));
            };

            this.dataMain = injectMeta(rawMain, type, 'standard', 'main');
            
            const pGames = injectMeta(sugGames, 'games', 'standard', 'sug-games');
            const pMovies = injectMeta(sugMovies, 'movies', 'standard', 'sug-movies');
            const pYt = injectMeta(sugYt, 'movies', 'youtube', 'sug-yt');

            const rawSuggestions = [...pGames, ...pMovies, ...pYt].map(item => ({...item, status: 'suggested'}));
            
            // Оставляем YouTube-видео в глобальном кэше всегда для новой страницы YouTube
            this.dataSuggestions = rawSuggestions.filter(item => item.type === type || item.format === 'youtube');
            
            this.fastPreprocess(this.dataMain);
            this.fastPreprocess(this.dataSuggestions);
                
            const allMedia = [...this.dataMain, ...this.dataSuggestions];
            AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'DATA_SET_MEDIA', payload: allMedia });

            this.processData();
            EventBus.emit('MEDIA_STORE_LOADED');
            
            this.fetchMissingTitles(allMedia, loadId);
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Ошибка загрузки медиа:", error);
            EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { type: 'system', tag: 'DATABASE', action: 'ERROR', value: `LOAD_${type.toUpperCase()}_FAILED`, color: '#ff4444' });
            
            if (this.currentLoadId === loadId) {
                this.activeLoadedType = null; 
                EventBus.emit('MEDIA_STORE_LOADED'); 
            }
        }
    }

    fastPreprocess(list) {
        for (const item of list) {
            if (item.format === 'collection') {
                this._normalizeCollection(item);
            }

            if (item.format === 'youtube' && item.videos && item.videos.length > 0) {
                const firstVid = item.videos[0]; 
                const ytId = getYouTubeId(firstVid);
                
                if (ytId && !item.image) {
                    item.image = `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${ytId}/hqdefault.jpg`;
                }
                if (!item.description) {
                    item.description = "Описание от зрителя отсутствует.";
                }
                if (!item.title) {
                    item.title = "Установка связи..."; 
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fetchMissingTitles(list, loadId) {
        let hasChanges = false;
        
        const fetchPromises = list.filter(i => i.format === 'youtube' && i.title === "Установка связи...").map(item => {
            const firstVid = item.videos[0];
            const fetchUrl = `https://www.youtube.com/oembed?url=${encodeURIComponent(firstVid)}&format=json`;
            
            return fetch(fetchUrl)
                .then(r => { if(!r.ok) throw new Error(); return r.json(); })
                .then(d => { if (d.title) { item.title = d.title; hasChanges = true; } })
                .catch(() => {
                    return fetch(`https://noembed.com/embed?url=${encodeURIComponent(firstVid)}`)
                        .then(r => r.json())
                        .then(d => { if (d.title) { item.title = d.title; hasChanges = true; } });
                })
                .catch(() => { item.title = "Засекреченный видеофайл"; hasChanges = true; });
        });

        if (fetchPromises.length > 0) {
            await Promise.allSettled(fetchPromises);
            
            if (this.currentLoadId !== loadId) return; 
            
            if (hasChanges) {
                AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'DATA_SET_MEDIA', payload: [...list] });
                this.processData();
                EventBus.emit('MEDIA_STORE_SILENT_UPDATE'); 
            }
        }
    }

    _normalizeCollection(item) {
        if (!item.items || item.items.length === 0) return;
        const firstItem = item.items[0];

        item.title = item.title || firstItem.title;
        item.description = item.description || firstItem.description;
        item.status = item.status || firstItem.status;
        item.customColor = item.customColor || firstItem.customColor;
        item.image = item.image || firstItem.image;

        if (!item.images) {
            item.images = item.items.slice(0, 3).map(sub => sub.image).filter(Boolean);
        }
        if (item.rating === undefined) {
            item.rating = firstItem.rating || 0;
        }

        item.stackColors = [
            item.customColor || '#444455',
            (item.items[1] && item.items[1].customColor) ? item.items[1].customColor : (item.customColor || '#444455'),
            (item.items[2] && item.items[2].customColor) ? item.items[2].customColor : (item.customColor || '#444455')
        ];
    }

    processData() {
        const { searchQuery, filters, sort, sortDirection } = AppStore.getState().media;
        const isAllSelected = filters.length === 0;

        const filterFn = (item) => {
            let matchScore = 1;
            
            if (searchQuery.length > 0) {
                let textToSearch = `${item.title || ''} ${item.description || ''}`.toLowerCase();
                if (item.format === 'collection' && item.items) {
                    item.items.forEach(sub => {
                        textToSearch += ` ${sub.title || ''} ${sub.description || ''}`;
                    });
                }
                matchScore = SearchEngine.scoreItemForGrid(textToSearch, item.title, searchQuery);
            }
            
            item._matchScore = matchScore;

            if (searchQuery.length > 0 && matchScore === 0) return false;
            if (isAllSelected) return true;

            return filters.includes(item.status);
        };

        let filteredMain = this.dataMain.filter(filterFn);
        let filteredSuggestions = this.dataSuggestions.filter(filterFn);

        const dir = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        const sortFn = (a, b) => {
            if (searchQuery.length > 0 && Math.abs((a._matchScore||0) - (b._matchScore||0)) > 0.1) {
                return (b._matchScore||0) - (a._matchScore||0);
            }
            if (sort === 'rating') {
                return ((a.rating || 0) - (b.rating || 0)) * dir;
            }
            return (a.title || "").localeCompare(b.title || "") * dir;
        };

        filteredMain.sort(sortFn);
        filteredSuggestions.sort(sortFn);

        this.filteredMain = filteredMain;
        this.filteredSuggestions = filteredSuggestions;
        this.combinedData = [...filteredMain, ...filteredSuggestions];

        // Отдаем в Основной Архив только стандартные карточки (Без YouTube)
        this.filteredMainStandard = filteredMain.filter(i => i.format !== 'youtube');
        this.filteredSuggStandard = filteredSuggestions.filter(i => i.format !== 'youtube');
        this.filteredYoutube = []; // Обнуляем, YouTube больше не нужен на главной странице
    }
}

/* js/music-player.js */
import { AudioCore } from './audio-core.js';
import { AudioUI } from './audio-ui.js';
import { AudioVisualizer } from './audio-visualizer.js';

let audioCoreInstance = null;
let audioUIInstance = null;
let audioVisualizerInstance = null;

export async function initMusicPlayer() {
    console.log('🎧 [MusicPlayer] Инициализация модуля...');
    
    // 1. Создаем ядро звука
    audioCoreInstance = new AudioCore();
    
    // 2. Дожидаемся загрузки плейлиста из API
    await audioCoreInstance.loadTracks();
    
    // 3. Создаем UI (передаем ему ядро для управления)
    audioUIInstance = new AudioUI(audioCoreInstance);
    
    // 4. Создаем визуализатор (передаем ему ядро для получения данных частот)
    audioVisualizerInstance = new AudioVisualizer(audioCoreInstance);
}

// Заглушка для обратной совместимости, если кто-то вызывает это извне
export function toggleMusicMode() {
    if (audioUIInstance) {
        audioUIInstance.toggleMode();
        return null;
    }
}

================================================================================

/* js/reptile-engine.js */

const SQUAD_COLORS = ["#ff4444", "#39ff14", "#ffd700", "#007bff", "#ff2d95"];

class Segment {
    constructor(parent, size, angle, range, stiffness) {
        this.isSegment = true;
        this.parent = parent;
        if (typeof parent.children === "object") parent.children.push(this);
        this.children = [];
        this.size = size;
        this.relAngle = angle;
        this.defAngle = angle;
        this.absAngle = parent.absAngle + angle;
        this.range = range;
        this.stiffness = stiffness;
        this.updateRelative(false, true);
    }
    updateRelative(iter, flex) {
        this.relAngle = this.relAngle - 2 * Math.PI * Math.floor((this.relAngle - this.defAngle) / 2 / Math.PI + 1 / 2);
        if (flex) {
            this.relAngle = Math.min(this.defAngle + this.range / 2, Math.max(this.defAngle - this.range / 2, (this.relAngle - this.defAngle) / this.stiffness + this.defAngle));
        }
        this.absAngle = this.parent.absAngle + this.relAngle;
        this.x = this.parent.x + Math.cos(this.absAngle) * this.size;
        this.y = this.parent.y + Math.sin(this.absAngle) * this.size;
        if (iter) this.children.forEach(c => c.updateRelative(iter, flex));
    }
    draw(ctx, iter) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(this.parent.x, this.parent.y); ctx.lineTo(this.x, this.y); ctx.stroke();
        if (iter) this.children.forEach(c => c.draw(ctx, true));
    }
    follow(iter) {
        const x = this.parent.x, y = this.parent.y;
        const dist = Math.hypot(this.x - x, this.y - y);
        this.x = x + this.size * (this.x - x) / dist;
        this.y = y + this.size * (this.y - y) / dist;
        this.absAngle = Math.atan2(this.y - y, this.x - x);
        this.relAngle = this.absAngle - this.parent.absAngle;
        this.updateRelative(false, true);
        if (iter) this.children.forEach(c => c.follow(true));
    }
}

class LimbSystem {
    constructor(end, length, speed, creature) {
        this.end = end; this.length = Math.max(1, length); this.creature = creature; this.speed = speed;
        creature.systems.push(this);
        this.nodes = [];
        let node = end;
        for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            this.nodes.unshift(node); node = node.parent;
            if (!node.isSegment) { this.length = i + 1; break; }
        }
        this.hip = this.nodes[0].parent;
    }
    moveTo(x, y) {
        this.nodes[0].updateRelative(true, true);
        let dist = Math.hypot(x - this.end.x, y - this.end.y);
        let len = Math.max(0, dist - this.speed);
        for (let i = this.nodes.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            let node = this.nodes[i];
            let ang = Math.atan2(node.y - y, node.x - x);
            node.x = x + len * Math.cos(ang); node.y = y + len * Math.sin(ang);
            x = node.x; y = node.y; len = node.size;
        }
        this.nodes.forEach(node => {
            node.absAngle = Math.atan2(node.y - node.parent.y, node.x - node.parent.x);
            node.relAngle = node.absAngle - node.parent.absAngle;
            node.children.forEach(childNode => {
                if (!this.nodes.includes(childNode)) childNode.updateRelative(true, false);
            });
        });
    }
    update(mouseX, mouseY) { this.moveTo(mouseX, mouseY); }
}

class LegSystem extends LimbSystem {
    constructor(end, length, speed, creature) { 
        super(end, length, speed, creature);
        this.goalX = end.x; this.goalY = end.y; this.step = 0; this.forwardness = 0;
        this.reach = 0.9 * Math.hypot(this.end.x - this.hip.x, this.end.y - this.hip.y);
        let relAngle = this.creature.absAngle - Math.atan2(this.end.y - this.hip.y, this.end.x - this.hip.x);
        relAngle -= 2 * Math.PI * Math.floor(relAngle / 2 / Math.PI + 1 / 2);
        this.swing = -relAngle + (2 * (relAngle < 0) - 1) * Math.PI / 2;
        this.swingOffset = this.creature.absAngle - this.hip.absAngle;
    }
    update(mouseX, mouseY) {
        this.moveTo(this.goalX, this.goalY);
        if (this.step === 0) {
            if (Math.hypot(this.end.x - this.goalX, this.end.y - this.goalY) > 1) {
                this.step = 1;
                this.goalX = this.hip.x + this.reach * Math.cos(this.swing + this.hip.absAngle + this.swingOffset) + (2 * Math.random() - 1) * this.reach / 2;
                this.goalY = this.hip.y + this.reach * Math.sin(this.swing + this.hip.absAngle + this.swingOffset) + (2 * Math.random() - 1) * this.reach / 2;
            }
        } else if (this.step === 1) {
            let theta = Math.atan2(this.end.y - this.hip.y, this.end.x - this.hip.x) - this.hip.absAngle;
            let forwardness2 = Math.hypot(this.end.x - this.hip.x, this.end.y - this.hip.y) * Math.cos(theta);
            let dF = this.forwardness - forwardness2;
            this.forwardness = forwardness2;
            if (dF * dF < 1) {
                this.step = 0;
                this.goalX = this.hip.x + (this.end.x - this.hip.x);
                this.goalY = this.hip.y + (this.end.y - this.hip.y);
            }
        }
    }
}

class Creature {
    constructor(x, y, angle, fAccel, fFric, fRes, fThresh, rAccel, rFric, rRes, rThresh) {
        this.x = x; this.y = y; this.absAngle = angle;
        this.fSpeed = 0; this.fAccel = fAccel; this.fFric = fFric; this.fRes = fRes; this.fThresh = fThresh;
        this.rSpeed = 0; this.rAccel = rAccel; this.rFric = rFric; this.rRes = rRes; this.rThresh = rThresh;
        this.children = []; this.systems = [];
    }
    follow(x, y, ctx) {
        let dist = Math.hypot(this.x - x, this.y - y);
        let angle = Math.atan2(y - this.y, x - this.x);
        let accel = this.fAccel;
        if (this.systems.length > 0) {
            let sum = this.systems.reduce((acc, sys) => acc + (sys.step === 0 ? 1 : 0), 0);
            accel *= sum / this.systems.length;
        }
        this.fSpeed += accel * (dist > this.fThresh);
        this.fSpeed *= 1 - this.fRes;
        this.speed = Math.max(0, this.fSpeed - this.fFric);
        
        let dif = this.absAngle - angle;
        dif -= 2 * Math.PI * Math.floor(dif / (2 * Math.PI) + 1 / 2);
        if (Math.abs(dif) > this.rThresh && dist > this.fThresh) {
            this.rSpeed -= this.rAccel * (2 * (dif > 0) - 1);
        }
        this.rSpeed *= 1 - this.rRes;
        if (Math.abs(this.rSpeed) > this.rFric) this.rSpeed -= this.rFric * (2 * (this.rSpeed > 0) - 1);
        else this.rSpeed = 0;
        
        this.absAngle += this.rSpeed;
        this.absAngle -= 2 * Math.PI * Math.floor(this.absAngle / (2 * Math.PI) + 1 / 2);
        this.x += this.speed * Math.cos(this.absAngle);
        this.y += this.speed * Math.sin(this.absAngle);
        
        this.absAngle += Math.PI;
        this.children.forEach(c => c.follow(true, true));
        this.systems.forEach(s => s.update(x, y));
        this.absAngle -= Math.PI;
        
        this.draw(ctx, true);
    }
    draw(ctx, iter) {
        const r = 4;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(this.x, this.y, r, Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle, 7 * Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle);
        ctx.moveTo(this.x + r * Math.cos(7 * Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle), this.y + r * Math.sin(7 * Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle));
        ctx.lineTo(this.x + r * Math.cos(this.absAngle) * 1.414, this.y + r * Math.sin(this.absAngle) * 1.414);
        ctx.lineTo(this.x + r * Math.cos(Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle), this.y + r * Math.sin(Math.PI / 4 + this.absAngle));
        ctx.stroke();
        if (iter) this.children.forEach(c => c.draw(ctx, true));
    }
}

// ООП Движок
export class ReptileEngine {
    constructor() {
        this.canvas = null;
        this.ctx = null;
        this.rafId = null;
        this.critter = null;
        
        this.mouse = { x: window.innerWidth / 2, y: window.innerHeight / 2 };
        
        // FPS Limiter
        this.lastTime = 0;
        this.fpsInterval = 1000 / 60; // Максимум 60 FPS

        this.onMouseMove = this.onMouseMove.bind(this);
        this.onResize = this.onResize.bind(this);
    }

    setupLizard(size, legs, tail) {
        const s = size;
        const critter = new Creature(window.innerWidth / 2, window.innerHeight / 2, 0, s * 9, s * 2, 0.45, 16, 0.4, 0.085, 0.5, 0.3);
        let spinal = critter;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            spinal = new Segment(spinal, s * 4, 0, Math.PI * 2 / 3, 1.1);
            for (let ii = -1; ii <= 1; ii += 2) {
                let node = new Segment(spinal, s * 3, ii, 0.1, 2);
                for (let iii = 0; iii < 3; iii++) node = new Segment(node, s * 0.1, -ii * 0.1, 0.1, 2);
            }
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < legs; i++) {
            if (i > 0) {
                for (let ii = 0; ii < 6; ii++) {
                    spinal = new Segment(spinal, s * 4, 0, 1.571, 1.5);
                    for (let iii = -1; iii <= 1; iii += 2) {
                        let node = new Segment(spinal, s * 3, iii * 1.571, 0.1, 1.5);
                        for (let iv = 0; iv < 3; iv++) node = new Segment(node, s * 3, -iii * 0.3, 0.1, 2);
                    }
                }
            }
            for (let ii = -1; ii <= 1; ii += 2) {
                let node = new Segment(spinal, s * 12, ii * 0.785, 0, 8); 
                node = new Segment(node, s * 16, -ii * 0.785, 6.28, 1); 
                node = new Segment(node, s * 16, ii * 1.571, 3.1415, 2); 
                for (let iii = 0; iii < 4; iii++) new Segment(node, s * 4, (iii / 3 - 0.5) * 1.571, 0.1, 4);
                new LegSystem(node, 3, s * 12, critter);
            }
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < tail; i++) {
            spinal = new Segment(spinal, s * 4, 0, Math.PI * 2 / 3, 1.1);
            for (let ii = -1; ii <= 1; ii += 2) {
                let node = new Segment(spinal, s * 3, ii, 0.1, 2);
                for (let iii = 0; iii < 3; iii++) node = new Segment(node, s * 3 * (tail - i) / tail, -ii * 0.1, 0.1, 2);
            }
        }
        return critter;
    }

    start() {
        if (this.canvas) this.stop();

        this.canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        document.body.appendChild(this.canvas);
        
        Object.assign(this.canvas.style, {
            position: "fixed", left: "0", top: "0",
            zIndex: "var(--z-system)", pointerEvents: "none", backgroundColor: "transparent"
        });
        
        this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext("2d");
        this.ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        this.onResize();

        const color = SQUAD_COLORS[Math.floor(Math.random() * SQUAD_COLORS.length)];
        this.ctx.strokeStyle = color;

        const legNum = Math.floor(1 + Math.random() * 8); 
        this.critter = this.setupLizard(8 / Math.sqrt(legNum), legNum, Math.floor(4 + Math.random() * legNum * 8));

        document.addEventListener("mousemove", this.onMouseMove);
        window.addEventListener("resize", this.onResize);

        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        console.log(`🦎 [ReptileEngine] Запущен. Цвет: ${color}. Лимит FPS: 60`);
        this.loop();
    }

    stop() {
        if (this.rafId) {
            cancelAnimationFrame(this.rafId);
            this.rafId = null;
        }
        if (this.canvas) {
            this.canvas.remove();
            this.canvas = null;
        }
        document.removeEventListener("mousemove", this.onMouseMove);
        window.removeEventListener("resize", this.onResize);
        console.log('🦎 [ReptileEngine] Остановлен');
    }

    onMouseMove(e) {
        this.mouse.x = e.clientX;
        this.mouse.y = e.clientY;
    }

    onResize() {
        if (!this.canvas) return;
        this.canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
        this.canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
    }

    loop() {
        this.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => this.loop());
        
        // --- FPS THROTTLE ---
        const now = performance.now();
        const elapsed = now - this.lastTime;

        if (elapsed < this.fpsInterval) return;
        this.lastTime = now - (elapsed % this.fpsInterval);

        // --- PHYSICS & DRAWING ---
        try {
            this.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.canvas.width, this.canvas.height);
            if (this.critter) this.critter.follow(this.mouse.x, this.mouse.y, this.ctx);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("🦎 [ReptileEngine] Ошибка в физическом цикле:", error);
            this.stop();
        }
    }
}

/* js/router.js */
import { AppStore } from './store.js';

class Router {
    constructor() {
        this.isInitialized = false;
    }

    init() {
        if (this.isInitialized) return;

        window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => this.handleHashChange());
        
        if (!window.location.hash || window.location.hash === '#') {
            window.location.hash = '#/';
        } else {
            this.handleHashChange();
        }

        this.isInitialized = true;
        console.log('🧭 [Router] Инициализирован и перехватывает маршруты');
    }

    handleHashChange() {
        try {
            const rawHash = window.location.hash.slice(1) || '/';
            const { path, query, params } = this.parseHash(rawHash);

            AppStore.dispatch({
                type: 'SET_ROUTE',
                payload: { path, params, query, raw: rawHash }
            });

            const mediaState = {
                searchQuery: query.search || '',
                filters: query.filters ? query.filters.split(',') : [],
                sort: query.sort || 'name',
                sortDirection: query.sortDir || 'asc',
                gridMode: query.mode || 'detailed'
            };
            
            if ((path === '/archive' || path === '/tierlist' || path === '/library') && params.type) {
                mediaState.type = params.type;
            }

            AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'MEDIA_SYNC_STATE', payload: mediaState });

            // Мы убрали логику модалки (query.modal === 'agent'), так как теперь это отдельная страница
            AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'MODAL_CLOSE' });

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('🚨 [Router] Ошибка при обработке маршрута:', error);
        }
    }

    parseHash(hash) {
        const [pathPart, queryPart] = hash.split('?');
        const query = {};
        
        if (queryPart) {
            const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(queryPart);
            for (let [key, value] of searchParams.entries()) {
                query[key] = value;
            }
        }

        const segments = pathPart.split('/').filter(Boolean);
        let path = '/';
        let params = {};

        if (segments.length > 0) {
            const base = segments[0];
            path = `/${base}`;
            
            if ((base === 'archive' || base === 'tierlist' || base === 'library') && segments[1]) {
                params.type = segments[1];
            } else if ((base === 'item' || base === 'agents') && segments[1]) {
                params.id = segments[1]; 
            }
        }

        return { path, query, params };
    }

    navigate(path, params = {}, query = {}) {
        let newHash = path;
        
        if ((path === '/archive' || path === '/tierlist' || path === '/library') && params.type) {
            newHash += `/${params.type}`;
        } else if ((path === '/item' || path === '/agents') && params.id) {
            newHash += `/${params.id}`; 
        }

        const queryKeys = Object.keys(query);
        if (queryKeys.length > 0) {
            const searchParams = new URLSearchParams();
            queryKeys.forEach(key => {
                if (query[key] !== null && query[key] !== undefined && query[key] !== '') {
                    searchParams.append(key, query[key]);
                }
            });
            const queryString = searchParams.toString();
            if (queryString) newHash += `?${queryString}`;
        }

        window.location.hash = newHash;
    }

    updateQuery(newQueryParams) {
        const currentHash = window.location.hash.slice(1) || '/';
        const { path, query, params } = this.parseHash(currentHash);
        
        const mergedQuery = { ...query, ...newQueryParams };
        
        Object.keys(mergedQuery).forEach(key => {
            if (mergedQuery[key] === null || mergedQuery[key] === undefined || mergedQuery[key] === '') {
                delete mergedQuery[key];
            }
        });

        this.navigate(path, params, mergedQuery);
    }
}

export const AppRouter = new Router();

/* js/schedule.js */
import { loadData } from './api.js';

export class ScheduleManager {
    constructor() {
        this.container = document.getElementById('schedule-container');
    }

    // ИСПРАВЛЕНО: Теперь 100% распознает дни независимо от того, написано полное слово или сокращение
    getDayIndex(dayStr) {
        const day = (dayStr || "").toLowerCase().trim();
        
        if (day.includes("воскресен") || day === "вс") return 0;
        if (day.includes("понедельник") || day === "пн") return 1;
        if (day.includes("вторник") || day === "вт") return 2;
        if (day.includes("сред") || day === "ср") return 3;
        if (day.includes("четверг") || day === "чт") return 4;
        if (day.includes("пятниц") || day === "пт") return 5;
        if (day.includes("суббот") || day === "сб") return 6;
        
        return 99; // Неизвестный день
    }

    async init() {
        if (!this.container) return;

        try {
            const data = await loadData('schedule.json', { schedule: [] });
            const segments = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.schedule || []);

            if (segments.length === 0) {
                this.container.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="schedule-empty">
                        <i class="fas fa-calendar-times" style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 10px; color: #444;"></i>
                        <br><span>БАЗА ПУСТА</span>
                    </div>`;
                return;
            }

            // Получаем текущий день недели от системы (0 - Воскресенье, 1 - Понедельник...)
            const currentDayIndex = new Date().getDay(); 

            this.container.innerHTML = segments.map(segment => {
                const dayStr = segment.day || "ТБА";
                const timeStr = segment.time || "--:--";
                const gameName = segment.game || "Секретная миссия";
                const streamTitle = segment.description || "";
                
                // Проверяем, совпадает ли день из JSON с сегодняшним днем
                const isToday = this.getDayIndex(dayStr) === currentDayIndex;

                let cardClass = 'sched-card';
                if (isToday) cardClass += ' is-today';
                else if (segment.highlighted) cardClass += ' is-highlighted';

                return `
                <div class="${cardClass}">
                    <div class="sched-indicator"><div class="sched-dot"></div></div>
                    <div class="sched-content">
                        <div class="sched-game">${gameName}</div>
                        ${streamTitle ? `<div class="sched-desc">${streamTitle}</div>` : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="sched-datetime">
                        <div class="sched-day">${dayStr.toUpperCase()}</div>
                        <div class="sched-time"><i class="far fa-clock"></i> ${timeStr}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
            
        } catch (error) {
            this.container.innerHTML = '<div class="network-error">ОШИБКА БАЗЫ ДАННЫХ</div>';
        }
    }
}

/* js/settings-menu.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';

export class SettingsMenu {
    constructor() {
        this.init();
    }

    init() {
        this.menuContainer = document.createElement('div');
        this.menuContainer.className = 'magic-menu-container';
        
        this.menuContainer.innerHTML = `
            <div class="magic-toggle" title="Меню"><i class="fas fa-cog"></i></div>
            <ul class="magic-items">
                <li style="--i:0;" title="Наверх"><a href="#hero"><i class="fas fa-arrow-up"></i></a></li>
                <li style="--i:1;" title="Убрать существ"><a href="#" id="btn-clear-creatures"><i class="fas fa-skull"></i></a></li>
                <li style="--i:2;" title="Режим Плеера"><a href="#" id="btn-toggle-music"><i class="fas fa-headphones"></i></a></li>
                <li style="--i:3;" title="YouTube Архив"><a href="#/youtube" id="btn-yt-lib" style="color: #ff0000; border-color: rgba(255,0,0,0.3);"><i class="fab fa-youtube"></i></a></li>
                <li style="--i:4;" title="Поддержать"><a href="#donation"><i class="fas fa-heart"></i></a></li>
            </ul>
        `;
        
        document.body.appendChild(this.menuContainer);
        
        this.toggleBtn = this.menuContainer.querySelector('.magic-toggle');
        this.btnClear = this.menuContainer.querySelector('#btn-clear-creatures');
        this.btnMusic = this.menuContainer.querySelector('#btn-toggle-music');

        this.setupToggleLogic();
        this.setupButtons();
        this.setupFooterCollision();
        
        console.log('⚙️ [SettingsMenu] Инициализировано и встроено в DOM');
    }

    setupToggleLogic() {
        this.toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            this.menuContainer.classList.toggle('active');
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!this.menuContainer.contains(e.target) && this.menuContainer.classList.contains('active')) {
                this.menuContainer.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });
    }

    setupButtons() {
        this.btnClear.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            EventBus.emit('UI_CLICK_CLEAR');
            this.triggerBtnFeedback(this.btnClear, 'var(--neon-pink)');
        });

        this.btnMusic.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'AUDIO_TOGGLE_MODE' });
        });

        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            if (state.audio.isMusicModeActive !== prevState?.audio?.isMusicModeActive) {
                if (state.audio.isMusicModeActive) this.btnMusic.classList.add('active-state');
                else this.btnMusic.classList.remove('active-state');
            }
        });
    }

    triggerBtnFeedback(btnEl, color) {
        btnEl.classList.add('active-state');
        btnEl.style.color = color;
        setTimeout(() => {
            btnEl.classList.remove('active-state');
            btnEl.style.color = '';
        }, 300);
    }

    setupFooterCollision() {
        const footer = document.querySelector('.cyber-footer');
        if (!footer) return;

        const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
            if (entries[0].isIntersecting) {
                this.menuContainer.classList.add('above-footer');
            } else {
                this.menuContainer.classList.remove('above-footer');
            }
        }, { threshold: 0.01 });

        observer.observe(footer);
    }
}
