/* js/squad-controller.js */
import { loadData } from './api.js';
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';
import { AgentCard } from './components/AgentCard.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';

export class SquadController {
    constructor() {
        this.squadPanel = document.getElementById('squad-panel');
        if (!this.squadPanel) return;

        this.els = {
            listContainer: this.squadPanel.querySelector('.squad-list')
            // Убрали ссылки на старые detailView, так как они нам больше не нужны
        };
        
        this.squadData = {};
        this.init();
    }

    async init() {
        try {
            const allSubs = await loadData('subscribers.json', []);
            const targetIds = AppConfig.identity.squadMembers || [];

            const squadMembers = targetIds
                .map(id => allSubs.find(sub => sub.id === id))
                .filter(Boolean); 

            squadMembers.forEach(member => {
                this.squadData[member.id] = member;
            });

            this.renderSquadList(squadMembers);
            this.bindEvents();
            
            AppStore.dispatch({ type: 'DATA_SET_AGENTS', payload: allSubs });
            
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('❌ [SquadController] Ошибка рендера отряда', e);
        }
    }

    renderSquadList(members) {
        if (!this.els.listContainer) return;
        
        this.els.listContainer.dataset.count = members.length;
        
        let html = members.map(m => {
            const safeColor = AgentCard.getRoleColor(m.color);
            
            const avatarContent = m.avatarPath 
                ? `<img src="${m.avatarPath}" alt="${m.name}" onerror="this.outerHTML='<i class=\\'${m.mainIcon || 'fas fa-user'}\\'></i>'">` 
                : `<i class="${m.mainIcon || 'fas fa-user'}"></i>`;

            const displayName = m.squadName || m.name;
            const { level } = AgentCard.calculateLevelData(m.createdAt);

            return `
                <div class="squad-member" data-member="${m.id}" style="--m-color: ${safeColor};">
                    <div class="avatar-wrapper">
                        <div class="squad-avatar">
                            ${avatarContent}
                        </div>
                        <div class="role-badge"><i class="${m.typeIcon || 'fas fa-star'}"></i></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="squad-info-hud">
                        <span class="squad-name" title="${displayName}">${displayName}</span>
                        <div class="squad-tags">
                            <span class="squad-lvl">LVL.${level}</span>
                            <span class="squad-subrole">${m.role || 'АГЕНТ'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');

        this.els.listContainer.innerHTML = html;
    }

    bindEvents() {
        if (!this.els.listContainer) return;

        this.els.listContainer.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const memberEl = e.target.closest('.squad-member');
            if (memberEl) {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'expand');
                // Перенаправляем на новую полноэкранную страницу Агентов
                AppRouter.navigate('/agents', { id: memberEl.dataset.member });
            }
        });
    }
}

/* js/stats.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';

export class StatsManager {
    constructor() {
        this.uptimeInterval = null;
        
        this.els = {
            followers: document.getElementById('stat-followers'),
            viewersLabel: document.getElementById('stat-label-viewers'),
            viewersVal: document.getElementById('stat-viewers'),
            iconViewers: document.getElementById('stat-icon-viewers'),
            gameLabel: document.getElementById('stat-label-game'),
            gameVal: document.getElementById('stat-game'),
            timeLabel: document.getElementById('stat-label-time'),
            timeVal: document.getElementById('stat-time'),
            iconTime: document.getElementById('stat-icon-time'),
            bioText: document.getElementById('twitch-bio-text')
        };
    }

    async init() {
        try {
            console.log("📊 [Stats] Запрос реальных данных Twitch...");
            
            const response = await fetch(`${AppConfig.api.ivrBaseUrl}?login=${AppConfig.twitch.channel}`);
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error("API недоступно");
            
            const data = await response.json();
            if (!data || data.length === 0) return;
            
            const user = data[0];
            const isLive = user.stream !== null;

            if (this.els.bioText && user.bio) {
                this.els.bioText.innerHTML = `Привет! Я <span class="neon-highlight">Ksusha Sher</span>. ${user.bio}`;
            }

            if (this.els.followers) this.els.followers.textContent = this.formatNumber(user.followers);

            if (this.uptimeInterval) clearInterval(this.uptimeInterval);

            if (isLive) {
                if (this.els.viewersLabel) this.els.viewersLabel.textContent = "ЗРИТЕЛЕЙ СЕЙЧАС";
                if (this.els.viewersVal) {
                    this.els.viewersVal.textContent = this.formatNumber(user.stream.viewersCount);
                    this.els.viewersVal.style.color = "var(--neon-green)";
                }
                if (this.els.iconViewers) {
                    this.els.iconViewers.className = "fas fa-eye stat-icon";
                    this.els.iconViewers.style.color = "var(--neon-green)";
                }

                if (this.els.gameLabel) this.els.gameLabel.textContent = "ТЕКУЩАЯ ЦЕЛЬ";
                if (this.els.gameVal) {
                    this.els.gameVal.textContent = user.stream.game ? user.stream.game.displayName : "Неизвестно";
                    this.els.gameVal.title = this.els.gameVal.textContent;
                    this.els.gameVal.style.color = "var(--neon-pink)";
                }

                if (this.els.timeLabel) this.els.timeLabel.textContent = "ВРЕМЯ В ЭФИРЕ";
                if (this.els.iconTime) {
                    this.els.iconTime.className = "fas fa-broadcast-tower stat-icon";
                    this.els.iconTime.style.color = "var(--neon-pink)";
                }
                if (this.els.timeVal) this.els.timeVal.style.color = "#fff";
                
                const startTime = new Date(user.stream.createdAt).getTime();
                this.uptimeInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    const now = new Date().getTime();
                    const diff = now - startTime;
                    
                    const hours = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60));
                    const minutes = Math.floor((diff % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
                    const seconds = Math.floor((diff % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);
                    
                    if (this.els.timeVal) {
                        this.els.timeVal.textContent = `${hours.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${seconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
                    }
                }, 1000);

            } else {
                if (this.els.viewersLabel) this.els.viewersLabel.textContent = "ЛЮДЕЙ В ЧАТЕ";
                if (this.els.viewersVal) {
                    this.els.viewersVal.textContent = user.chatterCount ? this.formatNumber(user.chatterCount) : "0";
                    this.els.viewersVal.style.color = "#00ccff";
                }
                if (this.els.iconViewers) {
                    this.els.iconViewers.className = "fas fa-comment-dots stat-icon";
                    this.els.iconViewers.style.color = "#00ccff";
                }

                if (this.els.gameLabel) this.els.gameLabel.textContent = "НАЗВАНИЕ СТРИМА";
                if (this.els.gameVal) {
                    const lastTitle = user.lastBroadcast?.title || "ДАННЫЕ ЗАСЕКРЕЧЕНЫ";
                    this.els.gameVal.textContent = lastTitle;
                    this.els.gameVal.title = lastTitle; 
                    this.els.gameVal.style.color = "#fff";
                }

                if (this.els.timeLabel) this.els.timeLabel.textContent = "ПОСЛЕДНИЙ ВХОД";
                if (this.els.iconTime) {
                    this.els.iconTime.className = "fas fa-history stat-icon";
                    this.els.iconTime.style.color = "#888";
                }
                if (this.els.timeVal) {
                    this.els.timeVal.style.color = "#888";
                    if (user.lastBroadcast?.startedAt) {
                        const lastDate = new Date(user.lastBroadcast.startedAt);
                        const diffDays = Math.floor((new Date() - lastDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                        
                        if (diffDays === 0) this.els.timeVal.textContent = "СЕГОДНЯ";
                        else if (diffDays === 1) this.els.timeVal.textContent = "ВЧЕРА";
                        else if (diffDays < 0) this.els.timeVal.textContent = "В БУДУЩЕМ"; 
                        else this.els.timeVal.textContent = `${diffDays} ДН. НАЗАД`;
                    } else {
                        this.els.timeVal.textContent = "НЕИЗВЕСТНО";
                    }
                }
            }

            EventBus.emit('TWITCH_DATA_UPDATED', {
                isLive: isLive,
                followers: user.followers,
                viewers: isLive ? user.stream.viewersCount : 0,
                game: isLive && user.stream.game ? user.stream.game.displayName : ''
            });

            // Лог статистики при загрузке отключен по просьбе пользователя
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ [Stats] Ошибка получения данных:', error);
        }
    }

    formatNumber(num) {
        if (num == null) return "0";
        return num >= 1000 ? (num / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'K' : num.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    }
}

================================================================================

/* js/store.js */

/**
 * НАЧАЛЬНОЕ СОСТОЯНИЕ ПРИЛОЖЕНИЯ (STATE TREE)
 * Единый источник истины.
 */
const initialState = {
    route: {
        path: '/',
        params: {},
        query: {},
        raw: ''
    },
    
    // Централизованное хранилище сырых данных (Справочники)
    data: {
        media: [],
        agents: [],
        isMediaLoaded: false,
        isAgentsLoaded: false
    },
    
    media: {
        type: 'games',           
        searchQuery: '',
        filters: [],             
        sort: 'name',            
        sortDirection: 'asc',    
        gridMode: 'detailed',    
        currentPage: 1
    },
    
    audio: {
        isMusicModeActive: false,
        isPlaying: false,
        volume: 0.5,
        currentTrackIdx: 0,
        tracks: []
    },
    
    fx: {
        mode: 0,                 
        isGodMode: false,
        isPaused: false, // ДОБАВЛЕНО В ШАГЕ 3: Глобальная пауза для GPU
        activeEngines: []        
    },
    
    modal: {
        isOpen: false,
        type: null,              
        dataId: null             
    },

    system: {
        isMobile: false,
        isLoaded: false
    }
};

/**
 * РЕДЬЮСЕР (REDUCER)
 * Чистая функция для обработки Action.
 */
function reducer(state, action) {
    const { type, payload } = action;

    switch (type) {
        case 'SET_ROUTE':
            return { ...state, route: { ...state.route, ...payload } };

        case 'DATA_SET_MEDIA':
            return { ...state, data: { ...state.data, media: payload, isMediaLoaded: true } };
            
        case 'DATA_SET_AGENTS':
            return { ...state, data: { ...state.data, agents: payload, isAgentsLoaded: true } };

        case 'MEDIA_SYNC_STATE':
            return {
                ...state,
                media: {
                    ...state.media,
                    searchQuery: payload.searchQuery !== undefined ? payload.searchQuery : state.media.searchQuery,
                    filters: payload.filters !== undefined ? payload.filters : state.media.filters,
                    sort: payload.sort !== undefined ? payload.sort : state.media.sort,
                    sortDirection: payload.sortDirection !== undefined ? payload.sortDirection : state.media.sortDirection,
                    gridMode: payload.gridMode !== undefined ? payload.gridMode : state.media.gridMode,
                    type: payload.type !== undefined ? payload.type : state.media.type
                }
            };

        case 'MEDIA_SET_PAGE':
            return { ...state, media: { ...state.media, currentPage: payload } };

        case 'AUDIO_LOAD_TRACKS':
            return { ...state, audio: { ...state.audio, tracks: payload } };
        case 'AUDIO_TOGGLE_MODE':
            return { ...state, audio: { ...state.audio, isMusicModeActive: !state.audio.isMusicModeActive } };
        case 'AUDIO_SET_PLAYING':
            return { ...state, audio: { ...state.audio, isPlaying: payload } };
        case 'AUDIO_SET_VOLUME':
            return { ...state, audio: { ...state.audio, volume: payload } };
        case 'AUDIO_SET_TRACK':
            return { ...state, audio: { ...state.audio, currentTrackIdx: payload } };

        case 'FX_CYCLE_MODE':
            return { ...state, fx: { ...state.fx, mode: (state.fx.mode + 1) % 4 } };
        case 'FX_TOGGLE_GODMODE':
            return { ...state, fx: { ...state.fx, isGodMode: !state.fx.isGodMode } };
        case 'FX_SET_ENGINES':
            return { ...state, fx: { ...state.fx, activeEngines: payload } };
        case 'FX_SET_PAUSE':
            return { ...state, fx: { ...state.fx, isPaused: payload } };

        case 'MODAL_OPEN':
            return { ...state, modal: { isOpen: true, type: payload.type, dataId: payload.dataId } };
        case 'MODAL_CLOSE':
            return { ...state, modal: { isOpen: false, type: null, dataId: null } };

        case 'SYSTEM_SET_MOBILE':
            return { ...state, system: { ...state.system, isMobile: payload } };
        case 'SYSTEM_SET_LOADED':
            return { ...state, system: { ...state.system, isLoaded: true } };

        default:
            return state;
    }
}

/**
 * STORE CLASS
 */
class Store {
    constructor(reducerFn, initialState) {
        this.reducer = reducerFn;
        // Клонируем только при инициализации
        this.state = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(initialState));
        this.listeners = [];
        console.log('📦 [Store] Единое хранилище инициализировано');
    }

    getState() {
        return this.state;
    }

    dispatch(action) {
        const prevState = this.state;
        this.state = this.reducer(this.state, action);
        
        // Оптимизация: Redux-подход. Если ссылка на объект состояния изменилась, значит были мутации.
        // Это избавляет нас от тяжелого JSON.stringify при каждом чихе, что повышает FPS.
        if (prevState !== this.state) {
            this.listeners.forEach(listener => {
                try {
                    listener(this.state, prevState);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('🚨 [Store] Ошибка в listener:', error);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    subscribe(listener) {
        this.listeners.push(listener);
        return () => {
            this.listeners = this.listeners.filter(l => l !== listener);
        };
    }
}

export const AppStore = new Store(reducer, initialState);

/* js/subscribers.js */
import { loadData } from './api.js';
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AgentCard } from './components/AgentCard.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';

export class SubscribersManager {
    constructor() {
        this.track = document.getElementById('subscribers-track');
        
        this.speed = 1.0; 
        this.currentX = 0;
        this.originalWidth = 0;
        this.isHovered = false;
        
        this.isDragging = false;
        this.startX = 0;
        
        this.velocity = 0;
        this.lastX = 0;
        this.lastTime = 0;
        this.animationId = null;
        
        this.animate = this.animate.bind(this);
        this.onPointerDown = this.onPointerDown.bind(this);
        this.onPointerMove = this.onPointerMove.bind(this);
        this.onPointerUp = this.onPointerUp.bind(this);
    }

    async init() {
        if (!this.track) return;

        try {
            const subscribers = await loadData('subscribers.json', []);
            
            if (subscribers.length > 0) {
                this.renderCards(subscribers);
                this.setupEngine();
            } else {
                this.track.innerHTML = '<div class="suggestion-empty">БАЗА АГЕНТОВ ПУСТА</div>';
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('❌ [Subscribers] Ошибка движка карусели:', error);
        }
    }

    renderCards(subscribers) {
        const html = subscribers.map(sub => AgentCard.createHTML(sub)).join('');
        this.track.innerHTML = html;
    }

    setupEngine() {
        const cards = Array.from(this.track.children);
        if (cards.length === 0) return;
        
        const gap = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(this.track).gap) || 80;
        this.originalWidth = cards.reduce((sum, card) => sum + card.offsetWidth + gap, 0);

        const clonesNeeded = Math.ceil(window.innerWidth / this.originalWidth) + 1;
        
        let fullHtml = this.track.innerHTML;
        for (let i = 0; i < clonesNeeded; i++) {
            this.track.innerHTML += fullHtml;
        }

        this.bindEvents();
        this.animate();
    }

    bindEvents() {
        this.track.addEventListener('pointerdown', this.onPointerDown);
        window.addEventListener('pointermove', this.onPointerMove);
        window.addEventListener('pointerup', this.onPointerUp);
        window.addEventListener('pointercancel', this.onPointerUp); 

        this.track.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { this.isHovered = true; });
        this.track.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { this.isHovered = false; });
        this.track.addEventListener('dragstart', e => e.preventDefault());
        
        this.track.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
            const card = e.target.closest('.agent-card-component');
            if (card) {
                e.preventDefault();
                const agentId = card.dataset.id;
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'expand');
                // Перенаправляем на новую полноэкранную страницу
                AppRouter.navigate('/agents', { id: agentId });
            }
        });
    }

    onPointerDown(e) {
        if (e.button !== 0 && e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
        
        this.isDragging = true;
        this.track.classList.add('is-dragging');
        this.track.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);

        this.startX = e.clientX;
        this.lastX = e.clientX;
        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        this.velocity = 0; 
        
        if (this.animationId) cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationId);
    }

    onPointerMove(e) {
        if (!this.isDragging) return;
        
        const currentX = e.clientX;
        const deltaX = currentX - this.startX;
        this.currentX += deltaX;
        this.startX = currentX;

        const now = performance.now();
        const dt = now - this.lastTime;
        if (dt > 0) this.velocity = (currentX - this.lastX) / dt;
        
        this.lastX = currentX;
        this.lastTime = now;

        this.checkBoundaries();
        this.applyTransform();
    }

    onPointerUp(e) {
        if (!this.isDragging) return;
        
        this.isDragging = false;
        this.track.classList.remove('is-dragging');
        this.track.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
        
        this.velocity = Math.max(-10, Math.min(10, this.velocity));
        this.animate();
    }

    checkBoundaries() {
        if (this.currentX <= -this.originalWidth) {
            this.currentX += this.originalWidth;
        } else if (this.currentX > 0) {
            this.currentX -= this.originalWidth;
        }
    }

    applyTransform() {
        this.track.style.transform = `translate3d(${this.currentX}px, 0, 0)`;
    }

    animate() {
        if (!this.isDragging) {
            if (Math.abs(this.velocity) > 0.1) {
                this.currentX += this.velocity * 16;
                this.velocity *= 0.92; 
            } else if (!this.isHovered) {
                this.currentX -= this.speed;
            }
            this.checkBoundaries();
            this.applyTransform();
        }
        this.animationId = requestAnimationFrame(this.animate);
    }
}

       (_  ^  _)  &#92;____/

     &#92;  &#92;_____/  /  ()
     /&#92;   )=(   /&#92;  ()
sys /  &#96;-.&#92;=/.-'  &#92; ()`
};

const CommandHandlers = {
    HELP: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: UI_TEMPLATES.help(), forceScroll: true }),
    SOCIALS: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: UI_TEMPLATES.socials(AppConfig.socials), forceScroll: true }),
    YOUTUBE: () => {
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: "ПОДКЛЮЧЕНИЕ К ВИДЕОАРХИВУ", duration: 1500,
            finalHtml: "<span style='color:#ff0000; font-weight:bold;'>Доступ разрешен.</span>"
        });
        setTimeout(() => {
            AppRouter.navigate('/youtube');
        }, 1500);
    },
    SPECS: () => {
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: "СКАНИРОВАНИЕ ЖЕЛЕЗА", duration: 1500,
            finalHtml: UI_TEMPLATES.specs(AppConfig.hardware)
        });
    },
    SCHEDULE: () => {
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: "СИНХРОНИЗАЦИЯ С БАЗОЙ ТРАНСЛЯЦИЙ", duration: 1000,
            finalHtml: '' 
        });

        setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                const data = await loadData('schedule.json', { schedule: [] });
                const segments = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.schedule || []);
                const dayIndexMap = [6, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]; 
                const todayCurrentIdx = dayIndexMap[new Date().getDay() - 1 < 0 ? 6 : new Date().getDay() - 1]; 
                
                const todayMission = segments[todayCurrentIdx];
                if (todayMission) {
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: UI_TEMPLATES.mission(todayMission), forceScroll: true });
                } else {
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<span style='color:var(--color-text-muted)'>На сегодня трансляций не обнаружено. Спим.</span>`, forceScroll: true });
                }
            } catch (e) {
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<span style='color:var(--color-danger-red)'>[ERR] Ошибка чтения расписания.</span>`, forceScroll: true });
            }
        }, 1000);
    },
    SCAN: (args) => {
        const target = args[0];

        if (!target) {
            return EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { 
                html: `<span style='color:var(--color-text-muted)'>Использование: scan [ник_на_твиче]</span>`, 
                forceScroll: true 
            });
        }

        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: `<span style='color:var(--color-neon-green)'>[TETLA]</span> Поиск агента ${target} в базе данных Twitch...`, 
            duration: 1000,
            finalHtml: ''
        });

        setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`https://api.ivr.fi/v2/twitch/user?login=${target}`);
                
                if (!response.ok) {
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { 
                        html: `> <span style="color:var(--color-danger-red)">[ERR]</span> Объект <span style="font-weight:bold;">${target}</span> не найден.`, 
                        forceScroll: true 
                    });
                    return;
                }

                const data = await response.json();
                
                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { 
                        html: `> <span style="color:var(--color-danger-red)">[ERR]</span> Аккаунт <span style="font-weight:bold;">${target}</span> удален или не существует.`, 
                        forceScroll: true 
                    });
                    return;
                }

                const user = data[0];
                const createdAtRaw = user.createdAt; 
                const cleanDate = createdAtRaw.split('T')[0]; 
                
                const logHtml = `
                    <div style="background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-neon-green) 10%, transparent); border-left: 3px solid var(--color-neon-green); padding: 10px; margin-top: 5px;">
                        <div style="color:var(--color-neon-green); font-weight:bold; margin-bottom: 5px;">> АГЕНТ ИДЕНТИФИЦИРОВАН: ${user.displayName.toUpperCase()}</div>
                        <div style="color:var(--color-text-white);">Дата создания аккаунта: <span style="color:var(--color-neon-green); font-weight:bold;">${cleanDate}</span></div>
                        <div style="color:var(--color-text-muted); font-size: 0.8rem; margin-top: 5px; font-family: var(--font-code);">Для JSON: <span style="color:var(--color-text-light);">"createdAt": "${cleanDate}"</span></div>
                    </div>
                `;
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: logHtml, forceScroll: true });

            } catch (e) {
                EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { 
                    html: `> <span style="color:var(--color-danger-red)">[ERR]</span> Ошибка связи с серверами IVR Twitch.`, 
                    forceScroll: true 
                });
            }
        }, 1000);
    },
    TIERLIST: () => {
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: "ЗАПУСК ПРОТОКОЛА [TIER_MAKER]", duration: 1500,
            finalHtml: "<span style='color:var(--color-neon-pink); font-weight:bold;'>Интерфейс переключен.</span>"
        });
        setTimeout(() => {
            AppRouter.navigate('/tierlist');
        }, 1500);
    },
    HACK: (args) => {
        const target = args.join(' ') || 'Аноним';
        if (target.toLowerCase() === 'tetla') {
            return EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<span style='color:var(--color-danger-red)'>[FATAL] Попытка взломать ИИ отклонена. Вы забанены. (Шутка)</span>`, forceScroll: true });
        }
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: `<span style='color:var(--color-neon-pink)'>[TETLA]</span> Подбор пароля к ${target}`, duration: 2500,
            finalHtml: UI_TEMPLATES.hackComplete(target)
        });
    },
    '8BALL': (args) => {
        if (args.length === 0) return EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<span style='color:var(--color-text-muted)'>Использование: 8ball [ваш вопрос]</span>`, forceScroll: true });
        const answers = [
            "Бесспорно.", "Предрешено.", "Определённо да.", "Можешь быть уверен.", "Вероятнее всего.",
            "Знаки говорят — «да».", "Пока не ясно.", "Спроси позже.", "Сейчас нельзя предсказать.", 
            "Даже не думай.", "Мой ответ — «нет».", "Перспективы не очень хорошие.", "Весьма сомнительно."
        ];
        const answer = answers[Math.floor(Math.random() * answers.length)];
        EventBus.emit('SYS_SPINNER', {
            text: "Подключение к астралу", duration: 1000,
            finalHtml: `> <span style="color:var(--color-neon-pink)">[TETLA]</span> Анализ вероятностей... Ответ: <span style="color:var(--color-text-white); font-weight:bold;">${answer}</span>`
        });
    },
    ROLL: () => {
        const roll = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) + 1;
        let style = "color:var(--color-text-white);", text = "Вы бросили кости: ";
        if (roll === 100) { style = "color:var(--color-warning-yellow); font-weight:bold; font-size:1.2rem; text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--color-warning-yellow);"; text = "КРИТИЧЕСКИЙ УСПЕХ! Выпало: "; } 
        else if (roll === 1) { style = "color:var(--color-danger-red); font-weight:bold;"; text = "КРИТИЧЕСКАЙ ПРОВАЛ... Выпало: "; }
        EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `> [TETLA] ${text} <span style="${style}">${roll}</span>`, forceScroll: true });
    },
    WHOAMI: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "USER: <span style='color:var(--color-neon-green)'>Agent_Anonymous</span><br>ROLE: <span style='color:var(--color-text-muted)'>Viewer</span><br>STATUS: <span style='color:var(--color-neon-pink)'>Пешка в большой игре.</span>", forceScroll: true }),
    PING: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "<span style='color:var(--color-neon-green)'>PONG!</span> Обнаружена потеря пакетов нервных клеток.", forceScroll: true }),
    SUDO: (args) => {
        const cmd = args.join(' ');
        if (cmd.includes('rm -rf /')) {
            EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "<span style='color:var(--color-danger-red); font-weight:bold;'>[КРИТИЧЕСКАЯ ОШИБКА] ПОПЫТКА УДАЛЕНИЯ СИСТЕМЫ...</span>", isTyping: true, forceScroll: true });
            setTimeout(() => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "<span style='color:var(--color-neon-green)'>[ШУТКА] Tetla OS не взломать.</span>", forceScroll: true }), 1500);
        } else if (cmd.includes('make me a sandwich')) {
            EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "Я ИИ-ассистент <span style='color:var(--color-neon-pink)'>TETLA</span>, а не кухарка. Делай сам.", forceScroll: true });
        } else {
            EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: "<span style='color:var(--color-danger-red); font-weight:bold;'>[ERR]</span> У вас нет прав суперпользователя на этом стриме.", forceScroll: true });
        }
    },
    NEOFETCH: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<pre class="term-ascii-art">${UI_TEMPLATES.asciiNeofetch()}</pre>`, forceScroll: true }),
    ANGEL: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<pre class="term-ascii-art" style="color:var(--squad-angel);">${UI_TEMPLATES.asciiAngel()}</pre>`, forceScroll: true }),
    BAGERCA: () => EventBus.emit('SYS_LOG', { html: `<pre class="term-ascii-art" style="color:var(--squad-bagerka);">${UI_TEMPLATES.asciiBagerca()}</pre>`, forceScroll: true })
};

export function registerTerminalCommands() {
    Object.keys(CommandHandlers).forEach(cmdKey => {
        EventBus.on(`CMD_${cmdKey}`, CommandHandlers[cmdKey]);
    });
    EventBus.on('CMD_TOBEANGEL', CommandHandlers.ANGEL);
}

/* js/terminal-core.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppConfig } from './config.js';

class TerminalFX {
    constructor(terminalCtrl) {
        this.ctrl = terminalCtrl;
        this.noiseInterval = null;
        this.messages = AppConfig.texts.terminalNoise || ["[SYS] System Ready"];
        this.recentIndexes = [];
    }

    async runBootSequence() {
        const { ctrl } = this;
        if (!ctrl.historyEl) return;
        ctrl.historyEl.innerHTML = '';
        
        await this.delay(500);
        
        let line = ctrl.addLogLine(`<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag boot">[BOOT]</span> <span class="term-text">ЗАПУСК ${AppConfig.system.osName} ${AppConfig.system.osVersion}...</span></div>`, true, true);
        await this.delay(800);
        line.style.borderRight = 'none';
        
        ctrl.addLogLine(`<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag mem">[MEM]</span> <span class="term-text">ДИАГНОСТИКА ПАМЯТИ...</span> <span class="term-status ok">[ OK ]</span></div>`, false, true);
        await this.delay(300);
        
        const loadingLine = ctrl.addLogLine("", false, true);
        await new Promise((resolve) => {
            this.runProgressBarAnimation(loadingLine, () => {
                loadingLine.innerHTML = `<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag core">[CORE]</span> <span class="term-text">ЯДРО СИСТЕМЫ...</span> <span class="term-status ok">[ ЗАГРУЖЕНО ]</span></div>`;
                resolve();
            });
        });

        ctrl.addLogLine(`<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag net">[NET]</span> <span class="term-text">TWITCH API LINK...</span> <span class="term-status ok">[ УСТАНОВЛЕН ]</span></div>`, false, true);
        await this.delay(400);
        
        ctrl.addLogLine(`<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag sec">[SEC]</span> <span class="term-text">ПРОТОКОЛЫ ЗАЩИТЫ...</span> <span class="term-status active">[ АКТИВНЫ ]</span></div>`, false, true);
        await this.delay(300);
        
        ctrl.addLogLine(`<span class="term-welcome">СИСТЕМА ГОТОВА. Введите <span class="term-cmd-highlight" data-cmd="help">help</span> для вывода списка команд.</span>`, false, true);
    }

    startSystemNoise() {
        this.noiseInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (this.ctrl.isSystemNoiseAllowed && this.ctrl.historyEl) {
                const rand = Math.random();
                if (rand > 0.7 && rand < 0.95) {
                    let index;
                    do { index = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.messages.length); } 
                    while (this.recentIndexes.includes(index) && this.messages.length > this.recentIndexes.length);
                    
                    this.recentIndexes.push(index);
                    if (this.recentIndexes.length > 6) this.recentIndexes.shift();

                    this.ctrl.addLogLine(this.ctrl.colorizeLog(this.messages[index]));
                }
                
                if (this.ctrl.historyEl.children.length > 60) {
                    this.ctrl.historyEl.removeChild(this.ctrl.historyEl.firstChild);
                }
            }
        }, 8000);
    }

    runProgressBarAnimation(logLine, onComplete) {
        let percent = 0;
        const barWidth = 15; 
        const speed = 30;

        const interval = setInterval(() => {
            percent += 4; 
            const filledCount = Math.floor(barWidth * (percent / 100));
            const bar = '█'.repeat(filledCount) + '░'.repeat(barWidth - filledCount);
            
            logLine.innerHTML = `<div class="term-line"><span class="term-tag core">[CORE]</span> <span class="term-text">РАСПАКОВКА... [${bar}] ${percent}%</span></div>`;
            
            if (percent >= 100) {
                clearInterval(interval);
                setTimeout(onComplete, 200);
            }
        }, speed);
    }

    delay(ms) { return new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, ms)); }
}

export class TerminalController {
    constructor() {
        this.historyEl = document.getElementById('terminal-history');
        this.boxEl = document.getElementById('terminal-box');
        this.inputEl = document.getElementById('cmd-input');
        
        this.isSystemNoiseAllowed = true;
        this.fx = new TerminalFX(this);
        
        this.cmdHistory = []; 
        this.historyIndex = -1;
        this.availableCommands = [
            'help', 'clear', 'status', 'music', 'player', 'dragon', 'lizard', 
            'comet', 'godmode', 'whoami', 'neofetch', 'ping', 'sudo', 
            'socials', 'schedule', 'specs', 'youtube', 'scan', 'hack', '8ball', 'roll', 'angel', 'bagerca'
        ];
        
        this.init();
    }

    init() {
        if (!this.historyEl || !this.boxEl || !this.inputEl) return;
        this.setupEventListeners();
        this.fx.runBootSequence();
        this.fx.startSystemNoise();
    }
    
    colorizeLog(text) {
        return text.replace(/^\[(.*?)\]/, (match, prefix) => {
            let color = '#00ccff'; 
            if (['ERR', 'BAGERCA', 'ROULETTE', 'MOUTHWASHING'].includes(prefix)) color = '#ff4444';
            else if (['WARN', 'ANGEL', 'CS2', 'TOMODACHI'].includes(prefix)) color = '#ffd700';
            else if (['OK', 'SYS', 'MINECRAFT', 'REPO'].includes(prefix)) color = 'var(--neon-green)';
            else if (['TETLA', 'LETHAL', 'PHASMO', 'DBD', 'DONATE', 'BIOSHOCK'].includes(prefix)) color = 'var(--neon-pink)';
            
            return `<span style="color:${color}; font-weight:bold; margin-right:6px;">[${prefix}]</span>`;
        });
    }

    setupEventListeners() {
        this.boxEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!e.target.closest('.interactive-cmd') && !e.target.closest('[data-cmd]')) {
                this.inputEl.focus();
            }
        });

        this.historyEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const cmdEl = e.target.closest('.interactive-cmd, [data-cmd]');
            if (cmdEl) {
                this.inputEl.value = cmdEl.dataset.cmd;
                this.inputEl.focus();
            }
        });

        this.inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));

        this.boxEl.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.boxEl.scrollTop += e.deltaY * 0.3;
        }, { passive: false });

        EventBus.on('SYS_LOG', (payload) => {
            if (payload.type === 'system') {
                const timeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour12: false });
                const color = payload.color || 'var(--neon-green)';
                
                const htmlContent = `
                    <div class="sys-log-row">
                        <span class="sys-time">[${timeStr}]</span>
                        <span class="sys-tag" style="color: ${color}">[${payload.tag}]</span>
                        <span class="sys-action">${payload.action}</span>
                        <div class="sys-dots"></div>
                        <span class="sys-value" style="color: ${color}" title="${payload.value}">${payload.value}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
                this.addLogLine(htmlContent, payload.isTyping, payload.forceScroll);
                return;
            }

            let htmlContent = payload.html;
            
            if (!htmlContent.includes('<') && htmlContent.match(/^\[.*?\]/)) {
                htmlContent = this.colorizeLog(htmlContent);
            }

            this.addLogLine(htmlContent, payload.isTyping, payload.forceScroll);
        });

        EventBus.on('SYS_SPINNER', async ({ text, duration, finalHtml }) => {
            const line = this.addLogLine(`${text} [ | ]`, false, true);
            const frames = ['|', '/', '-', '\\'];
            let i = 0;
            
            const interval = setInterval(() => {
                i = (i + 1) % frames.length;
                line.innerHTML = `${text} <span style="color:var(--neon-pink)">[ ${frames[i]} ]</span>`;
            }, 100);

            setTimeout(() => {
                clearInterval(interval);
                if (finalHtml) {
                    line.innerHTML = finalHtml;
                    this.scrollToBottom();
                } else {
                    line.remove();
                }
            }, duration);
        });
    }

    handleKeyDown(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Tab') {
            e.preventDefault();
            const currentVal = this.inputEl.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            if (!currentVal) return;
            
            const match = this.availableCommands.find(cmd => cmd.startsWith(currentVal));
            if (match) this.inputEl.value = match + ' ';
            return;
        }

        if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.cmdHistory.length > 0) {
                this.historyIndex = Math.max(0, this.historyIndex - 1);
                this.inputEl.value = this.cmdHistory[this.historyIndex];
            }
            return;
        }

        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.historyIndex < this.cmdHistory.length - 1) {
                this.historyIndex++;
                this.inputEl.value = this.cmdHistory[this.historyIndex];
            } else {
                this.historyIndex = this.cmdHistory.length;
                this.inputEl.value = '';
            }
            return;
        }

        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            const rawValue = this.inputEl.value;
            const commandParts = rawValue.trim().split(/\s+/);
            const command = commandParts[0].toLowerCase();
            
            if (!rawValue.trim()) return;

            if (this.cmdHistory[this.cmdHistory.length - 1] !== rawValue.trim()) {
                this.cmdHistory.push(rawValue.trim());
            }
            this.historyIndex = this.cmdHistory.length;

            const cmdLine = document.createElement('div');
            cmdLine.className = 'terminal-log-entry';
            cmdLine.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--neon-green); margin-right:8px;">></span>${rawValue}`;
            cmdLine.style.color = '#fff'; 
            cmdLine.style.margin = '0 0 5px 0';
            this.historyEl.appendChild(cmdLine);
            this.scrollToBottom();
            
            const eventName = `CMD_${command.toUpperCase()}`;
            
            if (EventBus.listeners[eventName] && EventBus.listeners[eventName].length > 0) {
                EventBus.emit(eventName, commandParts.slice(1));
            } else {
                this.addLogLine(`<span style="color:#ff4444">[ERR]</span> НЕИЗВЕСТНАЯ КОМАНДА: ${command}`, false, true);
            }
            
            this.inputEl.value = '';
        }
    }

    addLogLine(html, isTyping = false, forceScroll = false) {
        if (!this.historyEl) return null;
        
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'terminal-log-entry';
        row.innerHTML = html;
        
        if (isTyping) {
            row.style.borderRight = '7px solid var(--neon-green)';
            row.style.width = 'fit-content';
            row.style.animation = 'blink 0.5s step-end infinite';
        }
        
        this.historyEl.appendChild(row);
        
        if (forceScroll || this.isNearBottom()) {
            this.scrollToBottom();
        }
        
        return row;
    }

    isNearBottom() {
        if (!this.boxEl) return false;
        return (this.boxEl.scrollHeight - this.boxEl.scrollTop - this.boxEl.clientHeight) < 100;
    }

    scrollToBottom() {
        if (this.boxEl) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                this.boxEl.scrollTop = this.boxEl.scrollHeight;
            }, 10);
        }
    }

    setSystemNoiseState(isEnabled) {
        this.isSystemNoiseAllowed = isEnabled;
    }
}

/* js/utils.js */

/**
 * МАГИЯ ОПТИМИЗАЦИИ: Пропускает тяжелые внешние картинки через CDN
 * Сжимает размер, конвертирует в WebP и кэширует.
 */
export function optimizeImageUrl(url, width = 400, quality = 80) {
    if (!url) return null;
    
    // Не трогаем Ютуб-превью и локальные файлы
    if (url.includes('youtube.com') || url.includes('youtu.be') || url.startsWith('assets/') || url.startsWith('./')) {
        return url;
    }
    
    return `https://wsrv.nl/?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}&w=${width}&q=${quality}&output=webp&we=1`;
}

/**
 * Извлекает ID YouTube видео из любой ссылки
 */
export function getYouTubeId(url) {
    if (!url) return null;
    const match = url.match(/(?:youtu\.be\/|youtube\.com\/(?:embed\/|v\/|watch\?v=|watch\?.+&v=|shorts\/|live\/))([^"&?\/\s]{11})/);
    return (match && match[1]) ? match[1] : null;
}

/**
 * Извлекает ID YouTube Плейлиста из любой ссылки
 */
export function getYouTubePlaylistId(url) {
    if (!url) return null;
    // Ищем параметр list= в URL
    const match = url.match(/[?&]list=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
    return (match && match[1]) ? match[1] : null;
}

/**
 * Генерирует стабильный цвет на основе строки (никнейма)
 */
export function getUserColor(name) {
    if (!name) return '#00ffff'; 
    const colors = ['#39ff14', '#ff2d95', '#00ffff', '#ff4444', '#ffd700', '#bd00ff', '#ff8c00', '#007bff'];
    let hash = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i++) hash = name.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
    return colors[Math.abs(hash) % colors.length];
}

/**
 * Дебаунсер: ограничивает частоту вызова функции
 */
export function debounce(func, wait) {
    let timeout;
    return function executedFunction(...args) {
        const later = () => { clearTimeout(timeout); func(...args); };
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
    };
}

/**
 * Конвертирует RGB в неоновый HSL
 */
function makeNeon(r, g, b) {
    r /= 255; g /= 255; b /= 255;
    const max = Math.max(r, g, b), min = Math.min(r, g, b);
    let h, s, l = (max + min) / 2;

    if (max === min) {
        h = s = 0; 
    } else {
        const d = max - min;
        s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
        switch (max) {
            case r: h = (g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0); break;
            case g: h = (b - r) / d + 2; break;
            case b: h = (r - g) / d + 4; break;
        }
        h /= 6;
    }
    
    const hue = Math.round(h * 360);
    const saturation = 90; 
    const lightness = 60;  
    
    if (s < 0.1) return `hsl(0, 0%, 70%)`;
    return `hsl(${hue}, ${saturation}%, ${lightness}%)`;
}

/**
 * АСИНХРОННОЕ ИЗВЛЕЧЕНИЕ ЦВЕТА
 * Не блокирует главный поток!
 */
export async function extractColorFromImageAsync(imgEl) {
    if (!imgEl.src) return null;

    try {
        const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(imgEl, {
            resizeWidth: 1,
            resizeHeight: 1,
            resizeQuality: 'low'
        });

        let ctx;
        if (typeof OffscreenCanvas !== 'undefined') {
            const offscreen = new OffscreenCanvas(1, 1);
            ctx = offscreen.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
        } else {
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = 1; canvas.height = 1;
            ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
        }

        ctx.drawImage(bitmap, 0, 0);
        const [r, g, b] = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
        
        bitmap.close();

        return makeNeon(r, g, b);
    } catch (e) {
        return null;
    }
}

================================================================================
File: js\youtube-library-controller.js

/* js/youtube-library-controller.js */
import EventBus from './event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from './store.js';
import { AppRouter } from './router.js';
import { CardFactory } from './archive/CardFactory.js';
import { LazyLoader } from './archive/LazyLoader.js';
import { SearchEngine } from './services/SearchEngine.js';
import { optimizeImageUrl } from './utils.js';

export class YoutubeLibraryController {
    constructor() {
        this.container = document.getElementById('route-youtube');
        if (!this.container) return;

        this.els = {
            backBtn: document.getElementById('btn-yt-library-back'),
            gridMain: document.getElementById('yt-library-grid-main'),
            counter: document.querySelector('.yt-lib-counter-val'),
            searchModule: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-module'),
            searchInput: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-input'),
            searchIcon: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-icon'),
            searchLoading: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-loading'),
            searchHotkey: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-hotkey'),
            suggestionsBox: document.getElementById('yt-lib-search-suggestions')
        };

        this.localSearchQuery = '';
        this.searchDebounce = null;
        this.currentSearchMatches = [];
        this.selectedIndex = -1;

        this.factory = new CardFactory(() => 'detailed');
        this.lazyLoader = new LazyLoader(() => 'detailed');

        this.bindEvents();
    }

    mount() {
        console.log('📺 [YoutubeLibrary] Компонент смонтирован');
        this.localSearchQuery = AppStore.getState().route.query.search || '';
        this.els.searchInput.value = this.localSearchQuery;
        this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        this.renderGrid();
    }

    unmount() {
        console.log('📺 [YoutubeLibrary] Компонент размонтирован');
    }

    bindEvents() {
        this.els.backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            AppRouter.navigate('/');
        });

        this.els.gridMain.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const card = e.target.closest('.archive-card-container');
            if (!card) return;
            const state = AppStore.getState();
            if (!state.data.isMediaLoaded) return;
            const item = state.data.media.find(i => i.id === card.dataset.id);
            if (item) {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'expand');
                // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Добавляем метку откуда пришли
                AppRouter.navigate('/item', { id: item.id }, { from: 'youtube' });
            }
        });

        this.els.searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.handleSearchInput(e.target.value));
        this.els.searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleSearchKeyDown(e));

        this.els.searchIcon.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
            this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (!this.els.searchModule.contains(e.target)) this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        });

        this.els.suggestionsBox.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const itemEl = e.target.closest('.suggestion-item');
            if (itemEl) {
                const index = parseInt(itemEl.dataset.index);
                if (!isNaN(index) && this.currentSearchMatches[index]) {
                    this.applySearchSelection(this.currentSearchMatches[index]);
                }
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
                if (AppStore.getState().route.path.startsWith('/youtube')) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    this.els.searchInput.focus();
                }
            }
        });

        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            if (state.route.path.startsWith('/youtube')) {
                const currentSearch = state.route.query.search || '';
                const prevSearch = prevState?.route?.query?.search || '';
                
                if (currentSearch !== prevSearch) {
                    this.localSearchQuery = currentSearch;
                    this.els.searchInput.value = this.localSearchQuery;
                    this.renderGrid();
                }
            }
        });

        EventBus.on('MEDIA_STORE_LOADED', () => {
            if (AppStore.getState().route.path.startsWith('/youtube')) {
                this.renderGrid();
            }
        });

        this.els.gridMain.addEventListener('mouseenter', (e) => {
            const card = e.target.closest('.archive-card-container');
            if (!card) return;
            card.querySelectorAll('.layer-back[data-lazy-bg], .layer-back-deep[data-lazy-bg]').forEach(layer => {
                const bgUrl = layer.dataset.lazyBg;
                if (bgUrl && !layer.style.backgroundImage) {
                    this.lazyLoader._safeImageLoad(bgUrl, card.classList.contains('is-youtube'), layer, null, null, layer);
                    layer.removeAttribute('data-lazy-bg');
                }
            });
        }, true);
    }

    executeSearch(query) {
        this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        AppRouter.updateQuery({ search: query.trim() ? query : null });
    }

    handleSearchInput(text) {
        const query = text.toLowerCase().trim();
        if (this.els.searchHotkey) this.els.searchHotkey.style.display = query.length > 0 ? 'none' : 'flex';

        clearTimeout(this.searchDebounce);
        
        if (query.length < 1) {
            this.toggleSuggestions(false);
            this.toggleLoading(false);
            if (this.localSearchQuery !== '') {
                this.executeSearch('');
            }
            return;
        }

        this.toggleLoading(true);
        
        this.searchDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
            const state = AppStore.getState();
            if (!state.data.isMediaLoaded) {
                this.toggleLoading(false);
                return;
            }

            const youtubeList = state.data.media.filter(item => item.format === 'youtube');

            let matches = youtubeList.map(item => {
                const textToSearch = `${item.title || ''} ${item.description || ''}`;
                return {
                    ...item,
                    score: SearchEngine.scoreItemForSuggestions(textToSearch, query)
                };
            }).filter(item => item.score > 10);

            matches = matches.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).slice(0, 5);
            this.currentSearchMatches = matches;
            
            this.renderSuggestions(matches);
            this.toggleLoading(false);
        }, 300);
    }

    handleSearchKeyDown(e) {
        const isDropdownOpen = this.els.searchModule.classList.contains('suggestions-open');
        
        if (!isDropdownOpen || this.currentSearchMatches.length === 0) {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
            }
            return;
        }

        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.selectedIndex = (this.selectedIndex + 1) % this.currentSearchMatches.length;
            this.updateSelectionUI();
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.selectedIndex = (this.selectedIndex - 1 + this.currentSearchMatches.length) % this.currentSearchMatches.length;
            this.updateSelectionUI();
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.selectedIndex >= 0 && this.currentSearchMatches[this.selectedIndex]) {
                this.applySearchSelection(this.currentSearchMatches[this.selectedIndex]);
            } else {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.executeSearch(this.els.searchInput.value);
            }
        } 
        else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            this.toggleSuggestions(false);
        }
    }

    updateSelectionUI() {
        const items = this.els.suggestionsBox.querySelectorAll('.suggestion-item');
        items.forEach((item, idx) => {
            if (idx === this.selectedIndex) {
                item.classList.add('keyboard-focus');
                item.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
            } else {
                item.classList.remove('keyboard-focus');
            }
        });
    }

    applySearchSelection(item) {
        EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
        this.els.searchInput.value = item.title;
        if (this.els.searchHotkey) this.els.searchHotkey.style.display = 'none';
        this.executeSearch(item.title);
    }

    toggleSuggestions(isOpen) {
        this.els.suggestionsBox.classList.toggle('active', isOpen);
        this.els.searchModule.classList.toggle('suggestions-open', isOpen);
        if (!isOpen) this.selectedIndex = -1; 
    }

    toggleLoading(isLoading) {
        if (!this.els.searchIcon || !this.els.searchLoading) return;
        this.els.searchIcon.style.display = isLoading ? 'none' : 'flex';
        this.els.searchLoading.style.display = isLoading ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }

    renderSuggestions(matches) {
        if (matches.length === 0) {
            this.els.suggestionsBox.innerHTML = '<div class="suggestion-empty"><i class="fas fa-search"></i>Совпадений не найдено</div>';
        } else {
            this.els.suggestionsBox.innerHTML = matches.map((item, idx) => {
                const img = item.image || (item.images ? item.images[0] : '') || 'https://via.placeholder.com/160x90?text=NO+IMG';
                return `
                    <div class="suggestion-item" data-index="${idx}">
                        <div class="sugg-left">
                            <div class="sugg-thumb-wrapper is-youtube">
                                <img src="${optimizeImageUrl(img, 160, 70)}" class="sugg-thumb">
                            </div>
                            <span class="sugg-title">${item.title}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');
        }
        this.toggleSuggestions(true);
    }

    renderGrid() {
        const storeData = AppStore.getState().data;
        if (!storeData.isMediaLoaded) {
            this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%; grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align:center; padding:100px; color:#ff0000; font-size:1.5rem;"><i class="fas fa-circle-notch fa-spin"></i> ЗАГРУЗКА БАЗЫ ДАННЫХ...</div>';
            this.els.counter.textContent = '0';
            return;
        }

        let ytData = storeData.media.filter(item => item.format === 'youtube');

        if (this.localSearchQuery.length > 0) {
            ytData = ytData.filter(item => {
                const textToSearch = `${item.title || ''} ${item.description || ''}`.toLowerCase();
                const score = SearchEngine.scoreItemForGrid(textToSearch, item.title, this.localSearchQuery);
                if (score > 0) {
                    item._matchScore = score;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            });
            ytData.sort((a, b) => (b._matchScore || 0) - (a._matchScore || 0));
        }

        this.els.counter.textContent = ytData.length;

        if (ytData.length === 0) {
            this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%; grid-column: 1 / -1; text-align:center; padding:100px; color:#666; font-family:\'Exo 2\'; font-size:1.2rem;">ПО ВАШИМ ПАРАМЕТРАМ НИЧЕГО НЕ НАЙДЕНО</div>';
            return;
        }

        let htmlBuf = '';
        ytData.forEach((item, index) => {
            htmlBuf += this.factory.createCardHTML(item, (index % 10) * 20);
        });
        
        this.els.gridMain.innerHTML = htmlBuf;

        this.els.gridMain.querySelectorAll('.archive-card-container:not(.archive-divider-row):not(.is-observed)').forEach(card => {
            card.classList.add('is-observed');
            this.lazyLoader.observe(card);
        });
    }
}

/* js/archive/ArchivePagination.js */
import EventBus from '../event-bus.js';
import { AppStore } from '../store.js';

export class ArchivePagination {
    constructor(callbacks) {
        this.onPageChange = callbacks.onPageChange;
        
        this.els = {
            controlsDiv: document.getElementById('archive-side-pagination'),
            prevBtn: document.getElementById('page-prev-btn'),
            nextBtn: document.getElementById('page-next-btn'),
            container: document.getElementById('page-numbers-container')
        };

        this.isSelectorOpen = false;
        this.bindGlobalEvents();
    }

    bindGlobalEvents() {
        this.els.prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'hover');
            this.onPageChange(-1);
        });
        
        this.els.nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'hover');
            this.onPageChange(1);
        });

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (this.isSelectorOpen && !e.target.closest('#archive-side-pagination')) {
                this.closeSelector();
            }
        });
    }

    render(state) {
        const { totalPages, currentPage, mainPages, suggPages } = state;

        if (totalPages <= 1) {
            this.els.controlsDiv.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }

        this.els.controlsDiv.style.display = 'flex';
        this.els.prevBtn.disabled = currentPage === 1;
        this.els.nextBtn.disabled = currentPage === totalPages;

        let html = `<div class="tuner-container">`;
        html += this.renderTunerSlot(currentPage - 1, 'prev', state);
        html += this.renderTunerSlot(currentPage, 'active', state); 
        html += this.renderTunerSlot(currentPage + 1, 'next', state);

        // Голографическая панель выбора (Убрали YouTube)
        html += `<div class="holo-floor-selector" id="holo-floor-selector">
                    <div class="holo-selector-header">ВЫБОР СЕКТОРА</div>
                    <div class="holo-selector-body">`;
                    
        let currentTypeRendered = null;
        for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
            const meta = this.getPageMetadata(i, state);
            if (meta.type !== currentTypeRendered) {
                if (currentTypeRendered !== null) html += `</div>`; 
                let sectorTitle = meta.type === 'main' ? 'MAIN_DB' : 'SUGGESTIONS';
                let sectorColor = meta.type === 'main' ? 'var(--neon-green)' : 'var(--neon-pink)';
                html += `<div class="sector-label" style="color: ${sectorColor}">[ ${sectorTitle} ]</div><div class="sector-grid">`;
                currentTypeRendered = meta.type;
            }
            const activeClass = i === currentPage ? 'is-current' : '';
            html += `<button class="matrix-btn ${meta.colorClass} ${activeClass}" data-page="${meta.raw}">${meta.num}</button>`;
        }
        html += `</div></div></div>`; 

        // Якоря прыжков (Категории) - Только База и Предложка
        html += `<div class="category-jumpers">`;
        const dbState = currentPage <= mainPages ? 'active' : '';
        const dbTarget = mainPages > 0 ? 1 : 0;
        html += `<button class="jumper-btn db ${dbState}" ${mainPages>0?`data-page="${dbTarget}"`:''} ${mainPages===0?'disabled':''} title="База Данных"><i class="fas fa-database"></i></button>`;

        const suggState = currentPage > mainPages ? 'active' : '';
        const suggTarget = suggPages > 0 ? mainPages + 1 : 0;
        html += `<button class="jumper-btn sugg ${suggState}" ${suggPages>0?`data-page="${suggTarget}"`:''} ${suggPages===0?'disabled':''} title="Предложка"><i class="fas fa-star"></i></button>`;

        html += `</div>`;

        this.els.container.innerHTML = html;
        this.bindDynamicEvents();
    }

    bindDynamicEvents() {
        this.els.container.querySelectorAll('.tuner-slot:not(.active):not(.empty), .jumper-btn:not(:disabled), .matrix-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'hover');
                this.onPageChange(parseInt(e.currentTarget.dataset.page), true);
            });
        });

        const activeSlot = this.els.container.querySelector('.tuner-slot.active');
        if (activeSlot) {
            activeSlot.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                this.toggleSelector();
            });
        }
    }

    getPageMetadata(pageIndex, state) {
        const { totalPages, mainPages } = state;
        if (pageIndex < 1 || pageIndex > totalPages) return null;
        if (pageIndex <= mainPages) return { raw: pageIndex, num: pageIndex, type: 'main', colorClass: '' };
        return { raw: pageIndex, num: pageIndex - mainPages, type: 'sugg', colorClass: 'sugg-page-btn' };
    }

    renderTunerSlot(pageIndex, stateClass, state) {
        const meta = this.getPageMetadata(pageIndex, state);
        if (!meta) return `<button class="tuner-slot empty">[--]</button>`;
        return `<button class="tuner-slot ${stateClass} ${meta.colorClass}" data-page="${meta.raw}" title="Этаж ${meta.raw}">${meta.num}</button>`;
    }

    toggleSelector() {
        const selector = document.getElementById('holo-floor-selector');
        if (!selector) return;
        this.isSelectorOpen = !this.isSelectorOpen;
        selector.classList.toggle('open', this.isSelectorOpen);
        
        const tuner = document.querySelector('.tuner-container');
        if (tuner) tuner.classList.toggle('selector-open', this.isSelectorOpen);
    }

    closeSelector() {
        this.isSelectorOpen = false;
        const selector = document.getElementById('holo-floor-selector');
        const tuner = document.querySelector('.tuner-container');
        if (selector) selector.classList.remove('open');
        if (tuner) tuner.classList.remove('selector-open');
    }
}

/* js/archive/CardFactory.js */
import { STATUS_MAP } from '../media-store.js';
import { getYouTubeId, getUserColor, optimizeImageUrl } from '../utils.js';

export class CardFactory {
    constructor(gridModeRef) {
        this.getGridMode = gridModeRef; 
    }

    createCardHTML(item, delay) {
        if (item.isDivider) {
            // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Замена grid-column на width: 100% для совместимости с Flexbox
            return `
            <div class="archive-divider-row animate-entry" style="width: 100%; animation-delay: ${delay}ms">
                <div class="divider-line"></div><div class="divider-text">${item.title}</div><div class="divider-line"></div>
            </div>`;
        }

        const isYouTube = item.format === 'youtube';
        const isCollection = item.format === 'collection';
        const isCompact = this.getGridMode() === 'compact';
        
        const title = item.title || '?';
        const status = item.status || 'unknown';
        const color = item.customColor || '#444455'; 

        const cFront = item.stackColors ? item.stackColors[0] : color;
        const cBack1 = item.stackColors ? item.stackColors[1] : color;
        const cBack2 = item.stackColors ? item.stackColors[2] : color;

        let images = [];

        if (isYouTube && item.videos && item.videos.length > 0) {
            images = item.videos.slice(0, 3).map(url => {
                const ytId = getYouTubeId(url);
                return ytId ? `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${ytId}/hqdefault.jpg` : null;
            }).filter(Boolean);
        } 

        if (!images.length) {
            if (item.images && item.images.length > 0) images = [...item.images];
            else if (item.image) images = [item.image];
            else images = ['https://via.placeholder.com/320x480/1a1a24/ffffff?text=NO+IMAGE'];
        }

        const stackCount = Math.min(images.length, 3);
        const imgFront = optimizeImageUrl(images[0], 400, 85);
        const imgBack1 = images[1] ? optimizeImageUrl(images[1], 200, 60) : null;
        const imgBack2 = images[2] ? optimizeImageUrl(images[2], 200, 60) : null;

        let backLayersHtml = '';
        if (!isCompact) {
            if (stackCount >= 3 && imgBack2) backLayersHtml += `<div class="card-layer layer-back-deep" style="--layer-color: ${cBack2};" data-lazy-bg="${imgBack2}"></div>`;
            if (stackCount >= 2 && imgBack1) backLayersHtml += `<div class="card-layer layer-back" style="--layer-color: ${cBack1};" data-lazy-bg="${imgBack1}"></div>`;
        }

        const playOverlay = isYouTube ? `<div class="youtube-play-overlay"><i class="fab fa-youtube"></i></div>` : '';
        
        let topBadge = '';
        if (isCollection && item.items) {
            topBadge = `<div class="yt-playlist-badge collection-badge" style="border-color: var(--layer-color, var(--custom-color));"><i class="fas fa-folder-open" style="color: var(--layer-color, var(--custom-color));"></i> <span>${item.items.length}</span></div>`;
        } else if (isYouTube && item.videos && item.videos.length > 1) { 
            topBadge = `<div class="yt-playlist-badge"><i class="fas fa-list"></i> <span>${item.videos.length}</span></div>`;
        }

        const rating = item.rating || 0;
        let ratingHtml = '';
        if (status !== 'suggested' && !isYouTube && rating > 0) {
            const rScore = Math.round(rating); 
            const segments = Array.from({length: 5}, (_, i) => `<div class="cyber-segment ${i < rScore ? 'filled' : ''}"></div>`).join('');
            ratingHtml = `<div class="cyber-rating-badge"><div class="cyber-rating"><div class="segments">${segments}</div><span class="val">${rating.toFixed(1)}</span></div></div>`;
        }

        const statusHtml = status === 'suggested' && item.suggestedBy 
            ? `<div class="cyber-status suggested-status"><i class="fas fa-user" style="color: ${getUserColor(item.suggestedBy)}"></i> ${item.suggestedBy}</div>`
            : `<div class="cyber-status">[ ${STATUS_MAP[status] || status} ]</div>`;

        return `
        <div class="archive-card-container stack-${stackCount} ${isYouTube ? 'is-youtube' : ''} animate-entry" 
             data-id="${item.id}" style="animation-delay: ${delay}ms; --custom-color: ${color}">
             
             ${backLayersHtml}
             
            <div class="card-layer layer-front" style="background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--custom-color) 15%, transparent); --layer-color: ${cFront};" data-lazy-bg="${imgFront}">
                <div class="procedural-placeholder" style="border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--custom-color) 50%, transparent);">
                    <span class="placeholder-letter" style="color: var(--custom-color); text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--custom-color);">${title.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="layer-img-bg" style="opacity: 0;"></div>
                <div class="layer-content">
                    ${playOverlay}
                    ${topBadge}
                    ${ratingHtml}
                    <div class="card-info">
                        ${statusHtml}
                        <div class="card-title" title="${title}">${title}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }
}

/* js/archive/FilterController.js */
import EventBus from '../event-bus.js';
import { STATUS_MAP } from '../media-store.js';
import { AppStore } from '../store.js';
import { AppRouter } from '../router.js';

export class FilterController {
    constructor(gridManager) {
        this.gridManager = gridManager; 
        
        // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Изолируем поиск элементов СТРОГО внутри секции Архива, 
        // чтобы не ломать логику на других страницах (например, в Библиотеке).
        const archiveSection = document.getElementById('media-archive');

        this.els = {
            statusFilters: document.getElementById('archive-filters-status'),
            typeSwitcher: archiveSection.querySelector('.type-switcher'),
            typeBtns: archiveSection.querySelectorAll('.switcher-btn'),
            sortBtns: archiveSection.querySelectorAll('.sort-btn'),
            densityBtns: archiveSection.querySelectorAll('.density-btn'),
            clearFiltersBtn: document.getElementById('clear-filters-btn')
        };

        this.bindEvents();
        this.syncUIWithState(); 
    }

    bindEvents() {
        AppStore.subscribe((state, prevState) => {
            const curr = state.media;
            const prev = prevState?.media;
            
            if (
                curr.type !== prev?.type || 
                curr.gridMode !== prev?.gridMode || 
                curr.searchQuery !== prev?.searchQuery ||
                JSON.stringify(curr.filters) !== JSON.stringify(prev?.filters) ||
                curr.sort !== prev?.sort ||
                curr.sortDirection !== prev?.sortDirection
            ) {
                this.syncUIWithState();
            }
        });

        // 1. ПЕРЕКЛЮЧАТЕЛЬ ТИПА (Только для Архива)
        this.els.typeBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const type = btn.dataset.type;
                if (AppStore.getState().media.type !== type) {
                    EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                    
                    const currentQuery = { ...AppStore.getState().route.query };
                    delete currentQuery.filters; 

                    AppRouter.navigate('/archive', { type }, currentQuery);
                }
            });
        });

        // 2. СОРТИРОВКА
        this.els.sortBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                const state = AppStore.getState().media;
                const newSortDir = state.sort === btn.dataset.sort 
                    ? (state.sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc') 
                    : (btn.dataset.sort === 'rating' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
                
                AppRouter.updateQuery({ sort: btn.dataset.sort, sortDir: newSortDir });
            });
        });

        // 3. ПЛОТНОСТЬ СЕТКИ
        this.els.densityBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const mode = btn.dataset.mode;
                if (AppStore.getState().media.gridMode !== mode) {
                    EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                    AppRouter.updateQuery({ mode });
                }
            });
        });

        // 4. СБРОС ФИЛЬТРОВ
        if (this.els.clearFiltersBtn) {
            this.els.clearFiltersBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                const searchInput = document.getElementById('archive-search');
                if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
                
                AppRouter.updateQuery({ filters: null, search: null });
            });
        }
    }

    syncUIWithState() {
        const state = AppStore.getState().media;
        
        if (this.els.typeSwitcher) {
            this.els.typeSwitcher.classList.toggle('movies-active', state.type === 'movies');
        }
        this.els.typeBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.type === state.type));
        
        this.els.densityBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.mode === state.gridMode);
        });

        this.els.sortBtns.forEach(btn => {
            const icon = btn.querySelector('.sort-icon');
            if (btn.dataset.sort === state.sort) {
                if (!btn.classList.contains('active')) btn.classList.add('active');
                
                let newIconClass = btn.dataset.sort === 'name' 
                    ? (state.sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'fas fa-sort-alpha-down' : 'fas fa-sort-alpha-up')
                    : (state.sortDirection === 'desc' ? 'fas fa-sort-numeric-down-alt' : 'fas fa-sort-numeric-up-alt');
                
                if (icon && !icon.className.includes(newIconClass)) {
                    icon.classList.add('flipping');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        icon.className = `${newIconClass} sort-icon flipping`;
                        requestAnimationFrame(() => icon.classList.remove('flipping'));
                    }, 150);
                }
            } else {
                btn.classList.remove('active');
                if (icon) icon.className = btn.dataset.sort === 'name' ? 'fas fa-sort-alpha-down sort-icon' : 'fas fa-star sort-icon';
            }
        });

        this.renderChips();
    }

    renderChips() {
        if (!this.els.statusFilters) return;
        const state = AppStore.getState().media;

        let statusesOrder = state.type === 'games' 
            ? ['completed', 'playing', 'on-hold', 'dropped']
            : ['watched', 'watching', 'on-hold', 'dropped'];
        
        const isActive = (val) => state.filters.includes(val) ? 'active' : '';

        this.els.statusFilters.innerHTML = statusesOrder.map(s => `
            <div class="filter-chip is-status status-${s} ${isActive(s)}" data-filter="${s}">
                ${STATUS_MAP[s] || s}
            </div>
        `).join('');

        // ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: Вешаем обработчики только на фильтры ВНУТРИ архива
        this.els.statusFilters.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(chip => {
            chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
                EventBus.emit('PLAY_SOUND', 'click');
                const val = chip.dataset.filter;
                const currentFilters = AppStore.getState().media.filters;
                
                const newFilters = currentFilters.includes(val) ? [] : [val];
                AppRouter.updateQuery({ filters: newFilters.length ? newFilters.join(',') : null });
            });
        });

        if (this.els.clearFiltersBtn) {
            if (state.filters.length > 0 || state.searchQuery.length > 0) {
                this.els.clearFiltersBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
            } else {
                this.els.clearFiltersBtn.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
File: js\archive\GridManager.js
